晉書　附錄

## 修晉書詔

朕拯溺師旋，省方禮畢；四海無事，百揆多閑。遂因暇日，詳觀典府，考龜文於羲載，辨鳥冊於軒年。不出巖廊，神交千祀之外；穆然旒纊，臨睨九皇之表。是知右史序言，由斯不昧；左官詮事，歷茲未遠；發揮文字之本，通達書契之源，大矣哉，蓋史籍之爲用也！

自沮誦攝官之後，伯陽載筆之前，列代史臣，皆有刪著。仲尼修而採檮杌，倚相誦而闡丘墳。降自西京，班馬騰其茂實；逮于東漢，范謝振其芳聲。蕞爾當塗，陳壽敷其國志；眇哉劉宋，沈約裁其帝籍；至梁陳高氏，朕命勒成，惟周及隋，亦同甄錄：莫不彰善癉惡，激一代之清芬；褒吉懲凶，備百王之令典。

唯晉氏膺運，制有中原。上帝啓玄石之圖，下武代黃星之德。及中朝鼎沸，江左嗣興，並宅寰區，各重徽號，足以飛英麗筆，將美方書。但十有八家，雖存記注，而才非良史，事虧實錄。緒煩而寡要，思勞而少功；叔寧課虛，滋味同於畫餅；子雲學海，涓滴堙於涸流；處叔不預於中興，法盛莫通於創業；洎乎干陸曹鄧，略記帝王；鸞盛廣訟，<sub class="note-yuan">〔校注：「訟」當作「謙」，指劉謙之。說見余嘉錫四庫提要辨證卷三〕</sub>纔編載記。其文既野，其事罕傳，遂使典午清高，韜遺芳於簡冊；金行曩誌，闕繼美於驪騵。遐想寂寥，深爲歎息。

宜令修國史所更撰晉書，銓次舊聞，裁成義類。俾夫湮落之誥，咸使發明。其所須，可依五代史故事。若少學士，亦量事追取。貞觀二十年閏三月。

## 晉書音義

## 晉書音義序

晉書音義，余內弟東京處士何超字令升之所纂也。令升，即仲舅商州府君之子。惟我仲舅，實蘊多才。強學懿文，紹興門範；剖符行節，弘闡帝猷。雖位望兼崇，大名猶鬱，而增脩益振，餘慶方鍾。確爾專精，深期克復。時之未與，衣冠之嗣曷沈；道在則聞，儒素之風自遠。不隕其業，斯爲得歟！處士弟約以優閑，溺於墳史。嘗訝晉室之典，未昭其音，思欲發揮前人，啓迪後進。由是博考諸傳，綜覽羣言，研覆異同，撰成音義。亦足以暢先皇旨趣，爲學者司南。式敘其由，勸成其美。三都尚隱，思旌擅洛之文；五等迴封，遠愧平吳之績。巨唐天寶六載，天王左史弘農楊齊宣字正衡序。

晉書音義三卷

卷之上<sub class="note-yuan">紀志</sub>

卷之中<sub class="note-yuan">列傳上</sub>

卷之下<sub class="note-yuan">列傳及載記</sub>

先朝所撰晉書，帝紀十，志二十，列傳七十，載記三十，合一百三十篇。令升此音，紀、志共爲一卷，其列傳、載記各自區分，都成三軸，件目如左。仍依陸氏經典釋文注字，並以朱暎。服勤編簡，頗涉暄寒。凡所訓釋，必求典據，庶無牆面，疇敢師心。如或未周，敬俟來哲耳。

## 晉書音義卷之上<sub class="note-yuan">紀志</sub><sub class="note-yuan">起第一盡三十</sub>

## 帝紀第四卷<sub class="note-yuan">晉書四</sub>

翼<sub class="note-yuan">本或作翌，與翼同。</sub>楙<sub class="note-yuan">莫候反。本一作懋。</sub>泓<sub class="note-yuan">烏宏反。</sub>郝<sub class="note-yuan">好各反。</sub>奎<sub class="note-yuan">苦圭反。</sub>解系<sub class="note-yuan">胡計反。</sub>氐帥<sub class="note-yuan">所類反。</sub>禖<sub class="note-yuan">音梅。</sub>裴頠<sub class="note-yuan">魚毀反。</sub>皇孫𧇃<sub class="note-yuan">補閒反。</sub>冏<sub class="note-yuan">九永反。</sub>彗<sub class="note-yuan">似歲反，又似醉反。</sub>廞<sub class="note-yuan">許金反。</sub>汶山<sub class="note-yuan">音岷。</sub>堮<sub class="note-yuan">五各反。</sub>湨<sub class="note-yuan">公壁反，又補賴反。</sub>饕<sub class="note-yuan">吐高反。</sub>葛旟<sub class="note-yuan">音余。</sub>韡<sub class="note-yuan">韋鬼反。</sub>虓<sub class="note-yuan">許交反。</sub>炤<sub class="note-yuan">亦照字。之肖反。</sub>駝<sub class="note-yuan">徒何反。</sub>堨<sub class="note-yuan">烏葛反。</sub>碓<sub class="note-yuan">音對。</sub>涸<sub class="note-yuan">下各反。</sub>踧<sub class="note-yuan">子六反。</sub>周玘<sub class="note-yuan">音起。</sub>郫<sub class="note-yuan">音皮。</sub>圮<sub class="note-yuan">符鄙反。</sub>陳眕<sub class="note-yuan">字林曰：眕，目有所恨也。之忍反，又音真。</sub>鶡<sub class="note-yuan">音曷。</sub>齎<sub class="note-yuan">即兮反。</sub>粇<sub class="note-yuan">音庚。</sub>燥<sub class="note-yuan">蘇老反。乾也。</sub>盂<sub class="note-yuan">音于。</sub>歔欷<sub class="note-yuan">上音虛，下音許既反。</sub>暾<sub class="note-yuan">他昆反。</sub>馥<sub class="note-yuan">音復。</sub>繆胤<sub class="note-yuan">音謬。</sub>騫貙<sub class="note-yuan">敕居反。</sub>刁默<sub class="note-yuan">音彫。</sub>惤令<sub class="note-yuan">漢書惤音堅。[^8]</sub>爨<sub class="note-yuan">七亂反。</sub>跋<sub class="note-yuan">蒲撥反。</sub>蝦蟆<sub class="note-yuan">遐、麻二音。</sub>糜<sub class="note-yuan">靡爲反。</sub>鴆<sub class="note-yuan">直禁反。</sub>赧<sub class="note-yuan">奴版反。</sub>鼃<sub class="note-yuan">烏瓜反。蝦蟆也。又烏喎反。</sub>

## 帝紀第五卷<sub class="note-yuan">晉書五</sub>

螘衆<sub class="note-yuan">牛綺反。</sub>喁喁<sub class="note-yuan">字林曰：喁喁，衆口上見。魚容反。</sub>玫<sub class="note-yuan">莫迴反。</sub>尟<sub class="note-yuan">息淺反。</sub>鄄<sub class="note-yuan">音絹。</sub>惲<sub class="note-yuan">於粉反。</sub>錢璯<sub class="note-yuan">苦邁反。</sub>沶<sub class="note-yuan">音怡。</sub>枹鼓<sub class="note-yuan">音浮。本亦作桴。</sub>晞<sub class="note-yuan">音希。</sub>瑁<sub class="note-yuan">音妹。</sub>珉<sub class="note-yuan">武巾反。</sub>杜弢<sub class="note-yuan">他勞反。</sub>轘轅<sub class="note-yuan">還、袁二音。</sub>緄<sub class="note-yuan">古本反。</sub>疋<sub class="note-yuan">音雅。</sub>崧<sub class="note-yuan">音嵩。</sub>昵<sub class="note-yuan">尼質反。</sub>梟<sub class="note-yuan">古堯反。</sub>沌<sub class="note-yuan">音篆。</sub>髦<sub class="note-yuan">音毛。</sub>灄中<sub class="note-yuan">書涉反。</sub>骼<sub class="note-yuan">音格。</sub>胔<sub class="note-yuan">前智反。</sub>䴵<sub class="note-yuan">必郢反。</sub>磾<sub class="note-yuan">音低。</sub>螽<sub class="note-yuan">音終。</sub>薈<sub class="note-yuan">烏外反。</sub>喋<sub class="note-yuan">音牒。</sub>淇<sub class="note-yuan">音其。</sub>蹠<sub class="note-yuan">之石反。</sub>羯<sub class="note-yuan">居謁反。</sub>耒<sub class="note-yuan">盧對反。</sub>嫄<sub class="note-yuan">音元。</sub>貊<sub class="note-yuan">音陌。</sub>櫛<sub class="note-yuan">阻瑟反。</sub>枲<sub class="note-yuan">胥里反。</sub>弛<sub class="note-yuan">式是反。</sub>核<sub class="note-yuan">下革反。</sub>燮<sub class="note-yuan">蘇叶反。</sub>鼇<sub class="note-yuan">五勞反。</sub>獯<sub class="note-yuan">音勳。</sub>顱<sub class="note-yuan">力胡反。</sub>仆<sub class="note-yuan">音赴。</sub>

覲<sub class="note-yuan">渠刃反。</sub>藁<sub class="note-yuan">音杲。</sub>刈<sub class="note-yuan">音乂。</sub>煒如也<sub class="note-yuan">于鬼反。</sub>窘<sub class="note-yuan">其隕反。</sub>徼<sub class="note-yuan">工釣反。</sub>軼<sub class="note-yuan">音逸。</sub>擐<sub class="note-yuan">音患。</sub>鈇<sub class="note-yuan">音甫，又甫于反。</sub>毖<sub class="note-yuan">音祕。</sub>麗姬<sub class="note-yuan">與驪反。</sub>羿<sub class="note-yuan">五計反。</sub>荑<sub class="note-yuan">周易音蹄。</sub>浹辰<sub class="note-yuan">子協反。</sub>窬<sub class="note-yuan">音渝。</sub>逡<sub class="note-yuan">七旬反。</sub>郤<sub class="note-yuan">音隟。本或作郄，俗。</sub>悝<sub class="note-yuan">苦迴反。</sub>諝<sub class="note-yuan">私呂反。</sub>輯<sub class="note-yuan">音集。</sub>茹<sub class="note-yuan">而據反。</sub>贄<sub class="note-yuan">音至。</sub>惏<sub class="note-yuan">廬含反。</sub>珥<sub class="note-yuan">仍吏反。</sub>璠<sub class="note-yuan">音繁。</sub>龕<sub class="note-yuan">可含反。</sub>莞<sub class="note-yuan">胡管反，又音官。</sub>句驪<sub class="note-yuan">上古侯反，下音離。</sub>猒次<sub class="note-yuan">上一琰反，又一涉反。下如字，又音恣。</sub>紐<sub class="note-yuan">女九反。</sub>詮<sub class="note-yuan">此緣反。</sub>愞<sub class="note-yuan">乃亂反。一作懦。</sub>隗<sub class="note-yuan">五罪反。</sub>邃<sub class="note-yuan">雖遂反。</sub>郗<sub class="note-yuan">丑脂反。</sub>鄒<sub class="note-yuan">側鳩反。</sub>青綀<sub class="note-yuan">所居反。</sub>釵<sub class="note-yuan">初佳反。</sub>榼<sub class="note-yuan">苦盍反。</sub>貯<sub class="note-yuan">張呂反。</sub>扛鼎<sub class="note-yuan">音江。</sub>隳<sub class="note-yuan">許規反。</sub>齊斧<sub class="note-yuan">張晏曰：征伐斧也。以整齊天下也。張軌云：齊斧，蓋黃鉞也。應劭曰：齊，利也。虞憙志林云：[^9]齊當作齋，凡師出必齋戒入廟而受斧也。</sub>彝<sub class="note-yuan">音夷。</sub>鞅<sub class="note-yuan">於兩反。</sub>驤<sub class="note-yuan">音襄。</sub>嶲<sub class="note-yuan">息委反。</sub>釗<sub class="note-yuan">指遙反。</sub>跣<sub class="note-yuan">蘇典反。</sub>盱眙<sub class="note-yuan">上況于反，下與之反。</sub>篡<sub class="note-yuan">初宦反。</sub>公乘雄<sub class="note-yuan">公乘，姓也。見風俗通。</sub>巴滇<sub class="note-yuan">都賢反。</sub>嫗<sub class="note-yuan">衣遇反。</sub>壼<sub class="note-yuan">苦本反。</sub>朱雀桁<sub class="note-yuan">胡郎反。</sub>惋<sub class="note-yuan">烏亂反。</sub>爨<sub class="note-yuan">七亂反。</sub>李逷<sub class="note-yuan">他歷反。</sub>稷契<sub class="note-yuan">私列反。</sub>贇<sub class="note-yuan">於巾反。</sub>禋<sub class="note-yuan">音因。</sub>崧<sub class="note-yuan">音嵩。本亦作嵩。</sub>悆<sub class="note-yuan">羊茹反。</sub>曄<sub class="note-yuan">筠輒反。</sub>概<sub class="note-yuan">古代反。</sub>扞<sub class="note-yuan">音翰。</sub>推轂<sub class="note-yuan">吐迴切。</sub>圉<sub class="note-yuan">音語。</sub>緝<sub class="note-yuan">七入反。</sub>崎嶇<sub class="note-yuan">上去奇反，下音區。</sub>大祲<sub class="note-yuan">字林子沁反。</sub>縗絰<sub class="note-yuan">上音崔，下徒結反。</sub>箠<sub class="note-yuan">之累反。</sub>

## 帝紀第七卷<sub class="note-yuan">晉書七</sub>

迭<sub class="note-yuan">徒結反。</sub>涪<sub class="note-yuan">音浮。</sub>曹勱<sub class="note-yuan">音邁。</sub>㟭<sub class="note-yuan">音旻。</sub>劉闓<sub class="note-yuan">音鎧，又音開。</sub>枹罕<sub class="note-yuan">上甫于反，又音扶。下音漢。</sub>瑾<sub class="note-yuan">音覲。</sub>勰<sub class="note-yuan">音協。</sub>柤<sub class="note-yuan">側孤又側加反。</sub>燼<sub class="note-yuan">徐刃反。</sub>溧陽<sub class="note-yuan">音栗。</sub>上邽<sub class="note-yuan">音圭。</sub>珫<sub class="note-yuan">音充。本或作玩。[^10]</sub>毋丘<sub class="note-yuan">音無。</sub>塢<sub class="note-yuan">烏古。</sub>霍彪<sub class="note-yuan">甫休反。</sub>騶<sub class="note-yuan">側鳩反。</sub>皝<sub class="note-yuan">黃蕩反。</sub>郿<sub class="note-yuan">音眉。</sub>玝<sub class="note-yuan">音午。</sub>噲<sub class="note-yuan">苦怪反。</sub>凱<sub class="note-yuan">音愷。</sub>奎<sub class="note-yuan">苦圭反。</sub>桁<sub class="note-yuan">與航同。</sub>貉<sub class="note-yuan">音鶴。</sub>晞<sub class="note-yuan">音希。</sub>懌<sub class="note-yuan">音亦。</sub>降殺<sub class="note-yuan">所拜反。</sub>驎<sub class="note-yuan">力珍反。</sub>闐<sub class="note-yuan">音田。</sub>聃<sub class="note-yuan">他含反。</sub>瞿<sub class="note-yuan">音句。</sub>阽<sub class="note-yuan">音鹽。</sub>

## 帝紀第八卷<sub class="note-yuan">晉書八</sub>

瓚<sub class="note-yuan">昨旱反。</sub>燾<sub class="note-yuan">徒到反。</sub>爨頠<sub class="note-yuan">上七亂反，下魚毀反。</sub>儁<sub class="note-yuan">與俊同。</sub>畯<sub class="note-yuan">音俊。</sub>亢<sub class="note-yuan">剛、抗二音。</sub>氐帥<sub class="note-yuan">上丁奚反，下所類反。</sub>枋<sub class="note-yuan">音方。</sub>邯鄲<sub class="note-yuan">寒、丹二音。</sub>垣<sub class="note-yuan">音袁。</sub>𨔵<sub class="note-yuan">與遁同。</sub>芍陂<sub class="note-yuan">七削反。</sub>鮪<sub class="note-yuan">榮美反。</sub>萇<sub class="note-yuan">音長。</sub>靚<sub class="note-yuan">音淨。</sub>鴦<sub class="note-yuan">於良反。</sub>竺<sub class="note-yuan">音竹。</sub>佽飛<sub class="note-yuan">音次。</sub>鴆<sub class="note-yuan">直任反。</sub>曇<sub class="note-yuan">音潭。</sub>山茌<sub class="note-yuan">仕疑反。</sub>梁王㻱<sub class="note-yuan">子仁反。本亦作璡，下同。</sub>子暐<sub class="note-yuan">于鬼反。</sub>雛<sub class="note-yuan">士于反。</sub>汪<sub class="note-yuan">烏皇反。</sub>浩亹<sub class="note-yuan">上音閤，下音門。</sub>饋<sub class="note-yuan">逵位反。</sub>苛<sub class="note-yuan">音何。</sub>劉岵<sub class="note-yuan">音戶。</sub>餌<sub class="note-yuan">仍吏反。</sub>瓠<sub class="note-yuan">胡故反。</sub>赭圻<sub class="note-yuan">上之也反，下音祈。</sub>泯<sub class="note-yuan">武盡反。</sub>涂中<sub class="note-yuan">音途。[^11]</sub>虓<sub class="note-yuan">許交反。</sub>痿疾<sub class="note-yuan">字林：痿，痹也。人垂反，又於隹反。</sub>白帢<sub class="note-yuan">苦洽反。</sub>重閟<sub class="note-yuan">音祕。</sub>笫<sub class="note-yuan">莊几反，又側里反。</sub>禳<sub class="note-yuan">汝商反。</sub>僰<sub class="note-yuan">扶北反，又符逼反。</sub>

## 帝紀第九卷<sub class="note-yuan">晉書九</sub>

葆<sub class="note-yuan">音保。</sub>幘<sub class="note-yuan">音責。</sub>頤<sub class="note-yuan">與之反。</sub>勠<sub class="note-yuan">音六。</sub>秕政<sub class="note-yuan">補几、俾以二反，見國語。</sub>滑泥<sub class="note-yuan">上音骨。</sub>儋<sub class="note-yuan">都濫反。</sub>樂浪<sub class="note-yuan">洛、郎二音。</sub>卞眈<sub class="note-yuan">丁含反。</sub>赧<sub class="note-yuan">奴版反。</sub>寧康<sub class="note-yuan">一本云康寧。</sub>郁<sub class="note-yuan">於六反。</sub>天門蜑<sub class="note-yuan">徒旱反。蠻屬，見文字集略。或作蜒。</sub>墊江<sub class="note-yuan">音牒。</sub>邸<sub class="note-yuan">都禮反。</sub>句難<sub class="note-yuan">音鉤。</sub>璪<sub class="note-yuan">音早。</sub>逋<sub class="note-yuan">博孤反。</sub>句町<sub class="note-yuan">應劭：句音鉤。顏：町音挺。</sub>牢<sub class="note-yuan">音勞。</sub>阿房<sub class="note-yuan">如淳音旁。</sub>雹<sub class="note-yuan">蒲角反。雨冰也。</sub>龢<sub class="note-yuan">音和。</sub>螽<sub class="note-yuan">音終。</sub>忱<sub class="note-yuan">氏林反。</sub>潼<sub class="note-yuan">音同。</sub>湟<sub class="note-yuan">音皇。水名，在安定。</sub>晡<sub class="note-yuan">博孤反。</sub>臨<sub class="note-yuan">力禁反。</sub>惡<sub class="note-yuan">烏路反。</sub>醒<sub class="note-yuan">音星，又息定反。</sub>讖<sub class="note-yuan">初譖反。</sub>孕<sub class="note-yuan">以證反。</sub>屯<sub class="note-yuan">陟倫反。</sub>吒<sub class="note-yuan">陟嫁反。</sub>䉼<sub class="note-yuan">力弔反。一作料，本亦作断。</sub>彙<sub class="note-yuan">音謂。</sub>鬻<sub class="note-yuan">音育。</sub>鑣<sub class="note-yuan">甫驕反。</sub>

薊<sub class="note-yuan">音計。</sub>黁<sub class="note-yuan">奴昆反。</sub>炯<sub class="note-yuan">古迥反。</sub>阯<sub class="note-yuan">音止。</sub>沮渠<sub class="note-yuan">子余反。</sub>柵<sub class="note-yuan">惻戟反。</sub>嘏<sub class="note-yuan">音賈。</sub>騶<sub class="note-yuan">側鳩反。</sub>傉檀<sub class="note-yuan">內沃反。</sub>仄<sub class="note-yuan">阻力反。</sub>謐<sub class="note-yuan">音蜜。</sub>湓口<sub class="note-yuan">蒲奔反。水名，見尋陽記。</sub>崢嶸<sub class="note-yuan">上士耕反，下音宏。</sub>腆<sub class="note-yuan">他典反。</sub>柞<sub class="note-yuan">在各反，又音作。</sub>棱威<sub class="note-yuan">來登反。</sub>憝<sub class="note-yuan">徒對反。</sub>猖狂<sub class="note-yuan">上音昌。</sub>狡<sub class="note-yuan">古巧反。</sub>沮<sub class="note-yuan">慈呂反。</sub>孜<sub class="note-yuan">子之反。</sub>球<sub class="note-yuan">音求。</sub>跋<sub class="note-yuan">蒲撥反。</sub>臨朐<sub class="note-yuan">其俱反。</sub>鴟<sub class="note-yuan">處脂反。</sub>蒯<sub class="note-yuan">苦怪反。</sub>僖<sub class="note-yuan">許其反。</sub>眚<sub class="note-yuan">所景反。</sub>句驪<sub class="note-yuan">上古侯反，下音離。</sub>倭<sub class="note-yuan">烏和反。</sub>鵝<sub class="note-yuan">五歌反。</sub>縊<sub class="note-yuan">於賜反。</sub>衮<sub class="note-yuan">古本反。</sub>綟<sub class="note-yuan">盧結反。</sub>瞋目<sub class="note-yuan">充人反。字林云張目也。莊子云瞋目而語難。[^12]</sub>飪<sub class="note-yuan">如甚反。</sub>啟行<sub class="note-yuan">戶郎反。</sub>閫<sub class="note-yuan">苦本反。</sub>𡑞<sub class="note-yuan">音遂。</sub>有間<sub class="note-yuan">古莧反。</sub>屠<sub class="note-yuan">音徒。</sub>隻<sub class="note-yuan">之石反。</sub>燻<sub class="note-yuan">許云反。</sub>湎<sub class="note-yuan">彌兖反。</sub>

class="note-yuan">烏皎反。</sub>填星<sub class="note-yuan">陟刃反。</sub>魄焉<sub class="note-yuan">上音薄。</sub>覆冒<sub class="note-yuan">音副。</sub>聳<sub class="note-yuan">音悚。</sub>覆奩<sub class="note-yuan">甫于反。</sub>桴<sub class="note-yuan">甫于反。本作枹。</sub>潢<sub class="note-yuan">音黃。</sub>爲帝<sub class="note-yuan">于偽反。</sub>盛饘<sub class="note-yuan">上音成，下之然反。</sub>爟<sub class="note-yuan">音貫。</sub>鍵<sub class="note-yuan">音件。</sub>貉<sub class="note-yuan">音陌。</sub>觜觿<sub class="note-yuan">上子規反，下戶圭反。</sub>囷<sub class="note-yuan">去倫反。</sub>廥<sub class="note-yuan">丁禮反。</sub>枵<sub class="note-yuan">許喬反。</sub>諏<sub class="note-yuan">子侯反。</sub>樂浪<sub class="note-yuan">洛、郎二音。</sub>敦煌<sub class="note-yuan">屯、皇二音。</sub>牂柯<sub class="note-yuan">上子郎反，下音歌。</sub>

## 志第二卷<sub class="note-yuan">晉書十二</sub>

慝<sub class="note-yuan">吐得反。</sub>高埤<sub class="note-yuan">音卑。</sub>麓<sub class="note-yuan">音鹿。</sub>爍<sub class="note-yuan">書藥反。</sub>爲焠<sub class="note-yuan">七碎反。</sub>僇<sub class="note-yuan">音六。</sub>壅沮<sub class="note-yuan">上於容反，下七余反。壅本亦作雍。</sub>孛<sub class="note-yuan">音佩。</sub>格澤<sub class="note-yuan">如字。</sub>兌<sub class="note-yuan">音銳。本亦作銳。</sub>天欃<sub class="note-yuan">初銜反。</sub>雚<sub class="note-yuan">音丸。</sub>鏠<sub class="note-yuan">與鋒同。</sub>天柎<sub class="note-yuan">甫于反。</sub>綎<sub class="note-yuan">音庭。</sub>天榬<sub class="note-yuan">禹煩反。</sub>毚<sub class="note-yuan">士咸反。</sub>蓬絮<sub class="note-yuan">息據反。</sub>詘<sub class="note-yuan">區物反。</sub>十煇<sub class="note-yuan">音運，見周禮。</sub>眡祲<sub class="note-yuan">上音視，下子任反。</sub>僪<sub class="note-yuan">音決，又其聿反。本亦作璚。</sub>觿<sub class="note-yuan">子垂反，又戶圭反。本亦作鑴。</sub>瞢<sub class="note-yuan">莫登反，又莫亙反。</sub>璚<sub class="note-yuan">音瓊，見玉篇。</sub>亟<sub class="note-yuan">紀力反。</sub>叉<sub class="note-yuan">初牙反。</sub>杵<sub class="note-yuan">昌汝反。</sub>霾<sub class="note-yuan">莫皆反。</sub>蜄<sub class="note-yuan">時忍反。</sub>員簦<sub class="note-yuan">音登。</sub>闍<sub class="note-yuan">視奢反，又音都。</sub>𢁕<sub class="note-yuan">音鳥。[^13]</sub>蛪<sub class="note-yuan">音結，見蒼頡篇。</sub>瞀<sub class="note-yuan">莫候反。</sub>遹<sub class="note-yuan">音聿。</sub>隗<sub class="note-yuan">五罪反。</sub>仄匿<sub class="note-yuan">莊力反。</sub>眕<sub class="note-yuan">之忍反。</sub>狡<sub class="note-yuan">古巧反。</sub>皝<sub class="note-yuan">音晃。</sub>

## 志第三卷<sub class="note-yuan">晉書十三</sub>

楗閉<sub class="note-yuan">其輦反，又其偃反。</sub>甄氏<sub class="note-yuan">音真。</sub>虓<sub class="note-yuan">許交反。</sub>朐<sub class="note-yuan">音衢。</sub>鬣<sub class="note-yuan">良輒反。</sub>繆胤<sub class="note-yuan">音謬。</sub>砰<sub class="note-yuan">普耕反。</sub>弢<sub class="note-yuan">他高反。</sub>

class="note-yuan">音貞，又丈莖反。</sub>

## 志第六卷<sub class="note-yuan">晉書十六</sub>

侈弇<sub class="note-yuan">音奄。</sub>正日<sub class="note-yuan">音征。</sub>械<sub class="note-yuan">胡界反。</sub>徵<sub class="note-yuan">張里反。</sub>嘉量<sub class="note-yuan">力向反。</sub>籥合<sub class="note-yuan">藥、閤二音。</sub>伶<sub class="note-yuan">音零。</sub>竅<sub class="note-yuan">苦弔反。</sub>琯<sub class="note-yuan">音管。</sub>泠<sub class="note-yuan">音零。</sub>太蔟<sub class="note-yuan">音湊。</sub>洗<sub class="note-yuan">四典反，下同。</sub>無射<sub class="note-yuan">音亦。</sub>函鍾<sub class="note-yuan">[^17]音含。</sub>捃<sub class="note-yuan">居運反。</sub>廂<sub class="note-yuan">音襄。</sub>种整<sub class="note-yuan">之郢反。</sub>箱<sub class="note-yuan">音襄，本作廂。</sub>氂<sub class="note-yuan">力之反。</sub>有奇<sub class="note-yuan">居宜反。</sub>匏<sub class="note-yuan">白交反。</sub>哨<sub class="note-yuan">七曜反。小也，見考工記。[^18]又邪也，見廣雅。</sub>詖<sub class="note-yuan">彼義反。</sub>律中<sub class="note-yuan">張仲反。</sub>緹幔<sub class="note-yuan">徒奚反。赤色也。</sub>庳<sub class="note-yuan">音婢。下也。</sub>葭莩<sub class="note-yuan">加、孚二音。</sub>覆<sub class="note-yuan">音副。</sub>臬<sub class="note-yuan">魚列反。</sub>鬴<sub class="note-yuan">音父。</sub>臋<sub class="note-yuan">徒渾反。</sub>荅<sub class="note-yuan">都合反。</sub>絫<sub class="note-yuan">力委反。</sub>錘<sub class="note-yuan">直垂反。</sub>錙鐶<sub class="note-yuan">淄、還二音。</sub>碓<sub class="note-yuan">音對。</sub>

扐<sub class="note-yuan">音勒。</sub>撓<sub class="note-yuan">如紹反，又如效反。</sub>禨<sub class="note-yuan">居希反。</sub>差跌<sub class="note-yuan">上初皆反，下徒結反。</sub>三號<sub class="note-yuan">音豪。</sub>奇<sub class="note-yuan">居宜反。</sub>耦<sub class="note-yuan">五口反。</sub>

曀<sub class="note-yuan">烏計反。</sub>秕<sub class="note-yuan">卑履反。</sub>瑄<sub class="note-yuan">音宣。璧名。</sub>崧<sub class="note-yuan">音嵩。</sub>摭<sub class="note-yuan">之石反。</sub>駠<sub class="note-yuan">音留。赤馬黑鬣曰騮。</sub>䓇<sub class="note-yuan">與赫同。</sub>毳<sub class="note-yuan">昌芮反。</sub>菀窊<sub class="note-yuan">[^19]烏瓜反。</sub>笄<sub class="note-yuan">音雞。</sub>騩<sub class="note-yuan">音隗。馬淺黑色。</sub>壝<sub class="note-yuan">音位。壇也，周禮音惟。</sub>荀彧<sub class="note-yuan">於六反。</sub>禖<sub class="note-yuan">音梅。</sub>雩<sub class="note-yuan">音于。</sub>澍<sub class="note-yuan">之遇反。</sub>禜<sub class="note-yuan">音詠。</sub>幘<sub class="note-yuan">音責。</sub>灊<sub class="note-yuan">音潛。</sub>憝<sub class="note-yuan">徒對反。</sub>嘏<sub class="note-yuan">古雅反。</sub>葦茭<sub class="note-yuan">偉、交二音。</sub>梗<sub class="note-yuan">古杏反。</sub>磔<sub class="note-yuan">竹客反。</sub>煽<sub class="note-yuan">音扇。</sub>祧<sub class="note-yuan">他彫反。</sub>昭<sub class="note-yuan">音韶。</sub>嫄<sub class="note-yuan">音原。</sub>祫<sub class="note-yuan">音洽。</sub>窋<sub class="note-yuan">竹律反。</sub>墠<sub class="note-yuan">音善。</sub>醢<sub class="note-yuan">音海。</sub>剿<sub class="note-yuan">子小反。</sub>索綝<sub class="note-yuan">丑林反。</sub>

豐殺<sub class="note-yuan">所拜反。</sub>苴<sub class="note-yuan">子余反。</sub>絰<sub class="note-yuan">大結反。</sub>煢<sub class="note-yuan">音瓊。</sub>衰<sub class="note-yuan">音崔。</sub>軺輦<sub class="note-yuan">音遙。</sub>晡<sub class="note-yuan">補胡反。</sub>臨<sub class="note-yuan">力禁反。</sub>險易<sub class="note-yuan">以豉反。</sub>洿<sub class="note-yuan">音烏。</sub>江虨<sub class="note-yuan">甫巾反，又方閑反。</sub>齊斬<sub class="note-yuan">上音資。</sub>燕<sub class="note-yuan">音宴。</sub>咺<sub class="note-yuan">況晚反。</sub>賵<sub class="note-yuan">芳鳳反。</sub>塊<sub class="note-yuan">苦對反。</sub>厭降<sub class="note-yuan">一葉反。</sub>倅<sub class="note-yuan">七碎反。</sub>號咷<sub class="note-yuan">豪、陶二音。</sub>匍匐<sub class="note-yuan">上音蒲，下蒲北反。</sub>武賁<sub class="note-yuan">音奔。</sub>楯<sub class="note-yuan">食允反。</sub>鹵簿<sub class="note-yuan">上音魯，下裴古反。</sub>蠆<sub class="note-yuan">丑芥反。</sub>輓<sub class="note-yuan">音万。</sub>枚<sub class="note-yuan">音梅。</sub>裼<sub class="note-yuan">[^20]先擊反。</sub>屠蕢<sub class="note-yuan">苦壞反，本作蒯。</sub>篋<sub class="note-yuan">苦協反。</sub>識<sub class="note-yuan">音志。</sub>珥<sub class="note-yuan">仍吏反。</sub>椑<sub class="note-yuan">蒲歷反，又音闢。</sub>毖<sub class="note-yuan">音祕。</sub>悝<sub class="note-yuan">苦回反。</sub>恧<sub class="note-yuan">女六反。</sub>五員<sub class="note-yuan">音云。</sub>析<sub class="note-yuan">音錫。</sub>礿<sub class="note-yuan">音藥。</sub>

正會<sub class="note-yuan">音征。</sub>贄<sub class="note-yuan">音至。</sub>百華<sub class="note-yuan">音花。</sub>跪<sub class="note-yuan">去委反，又求累反。</sub>壝<sub class="note-yuan">以水反，亦音遺。</sub>旂<sub class="note-yuan">音祈。</sub>燔<sub class="note-yuan">音繁。</sub>禰<sub class="note-yuan">乃禮反。</sub>跳<sub class="note-yuan">音條。</sub>瞽叟<sub class="note-yuan">上音古，下素口反。</sub>逌<sub class="note-yuan">音由。</sub>壼<sub class="note-yuan">苦本反。</sub>褠幘<sub class="note-yuan">鉤、責二音。</sub>勱<sub class="note-yuan">音邁。</sub>孟縶<sub class="note-yuan">張立反。</sub>宿縣<sub class="note-yuan">音懸。本作懸。</sub>杕杜<sub class="note-yuan">音悌。</sub>鬣<sub class="note-yuan">良涉反。</sub>麛<sub class="note-yuan">音迷。</sub>𤠾劉<sub class="note-yuan">敕俱反，本亦作貙。</sub>肄<sub class="note-yuan">羊至反。</sub>阼<sub class="note-yuan">音祚。東階。</sub>醮<sub class="note-yuan">子笑反。</sub>華廙<sub class="note-yuan">音翼。</sub>祺<sub class="note-yuan">音其。</sub>紞<sub class="note-yuan">都敢反。</sub>纁<sub class="note-yuan">音勳。</sub>整<sub class="note-yuan">之郢反。</sub>罼<sub class="note-yuan">音必。</sub>雩婁侯<sub class="note-yuan">即令升之十五代祖。雩音況于反。</sub>悸<sub class="note-yuan">其季反。</sub>禊<sub class="note-yuan">音系。</sub>祓除<sub class="note-yuan">音弗。</sub>

氂<sub class="note-yuan">力之反。</sub>齊斧<sub class="note-yuan">如字，亦音資。解已見上文。</sub>鉦<sub class="note-yuan">音征。鐃也，似鈴。</sub>鐃<sub class="note-yuan">女交反。</sub>繆<sub class="note-yuan">音謬。</sub>攄<sub class="note-yuan">敕居反。</sub>皖<sub class="note-yuan">胡管反。</sub>獮<sub class="note-yuan">息淺反。</sub>痡<sub class="note-yuan">普胡反，又音孚。</sub>礫<sub class="note-yuan">音歷。</sub>螭<sub class="note-yuan">丑之反。</sub>麓<sub class="note-yuan">音鹿。</sub>闞<sub class="note-yuan">火斬反，又火檻反。</sub>鍠鍠<sub class="note-yuan">協韵音皇。</sub>禡<sub class="note-yuan">莫駕反。</sub>祊<sub class="note-yuan">甫彭反。</sub>鐲<sub class="note-yuan">音蜀，又音濁。</sub>蜿蜿<sub class="note-yuan">音婉。</sub>翽翽<sub class="note-yuan">呼外反。</sub>髦才<sub class="note-yuan">音毛。</sub>酷祝<sub class="note-yuan">之六反。</sub>鞞<sub class="note-yuan">蒲迷反。亦作鼙。</sub>煨<sub class="note-yuan">烏回反。</sub>哮闞<sub class="note-yuan">上許交反，下火斬反。</sub>慴<sub class="note-yuan">之涉反。</sub>茨<sub class="note-yuan">疾資反。</sub>身患<sub class="note-yuan">協韵音還。</sub>削中<sub class="note-yuan">音肖。</sub>嚙<sub class="note-yuan">五結反。</sub>嶋<sub class="note-yuan">都浩反。</sub>耨鑮<sub class="note-yuan">字當作鎛，鉏屬也。補各反。</sub>凘<sub class="note-yuan">音斯。</sub>綆<sub class="note-yuan">古杏反。</sub>鼖<sub class="note-yuan">符云反。</sub>魑魅<sub class="note-yuan">上丑知反，下音媚。</sub>兜<sub class="note-yuan">當侯反。</sub>郝<sub class="note-yuan">好各反。</sub>珉<sub class="note-yuan">音旻。</sub>懊憹<sub class="note-yuan">上烏浩反，下奴浩反。</sub>廞<sub class="note-yuan">許金反。</sub>柈<sub class="note-yuan">音盤。</sub>紵<sub class="note-yuan">音佇。</sub>嗽<sub class="note-yuan">蘇豆反。</sub>扛<sub class="note-yuan">音江。</sub>絚<sub class="note-yuan">古恆反。</sub>跂<sub class="note-yuan">音岐。</sub>棬<sub class="note-yuan">音權。</sub>笮<sub class="note-yuan">側陌反，又音祚。</sub>

纁<sub class="note-yuan">音勳。</sub>赬<sub class="note-yuan">敕貞反。</sub>鞙<sub class="note-yuan">胡犬反。</sub>韍<sub class="note-yuan">分物反。</sub>杓<sub class="note-yuan">音標。</sub>鞗<sub class="note-yuan">許縛反。</sub>軔<sub class="note-yuan">音刃。</sub>葆<sub class="note-yuan">音保。</sub>儐<sub class="note-yuan">必刃反。</sub>地正<sub class="note-yuan">音征。</sub>𣝛<sub class="note-yuan">音同。</sub>橑<sub class="note-yuan">音老。</sub>玳瑁<sub class="note-yuan">代、冒二音。</sub>鵾翅<sub class="note-yuan">上音昆，下施智反。</sub>棨<sub class="note-yuan">音啟。</sub>橐<sub class="note-yuan">戶郎反。</sub>𢱫<sub class="note-yuan">音岡。舉也。</sub>邪拖<sub class="note-yuan">託何反。</sub>鞉<sub class="note-yuan">音陶。</sub>𢄻<sub class="note-yuan">蘇但反。</sub>矟<sub class="note-yuan">山卓反。</sub>袴褶<sub class="note-yuan">神入反。</sub>較<sub class="note-yuan">音角。</sub>蚤<sub class="note-yuan">音早。</sub>轓<sub class="note-yuan">音藩，下同。</sub>幰<sub class="note-yuan">虛偃反。</sub>傳乘<sub class="note-yuan">張戀反。</sub>輜<sub class="note-yuan">側持反。</sub>軿<sub class="note-yuan">蒲丁反，又音蠙。</sub>騩<sub class="note-yuan">音愧。淺黑色。</sub>歐<sub class="note-yuan">烏侯反。</sub>縹紺<sub class="note-yuan">上敷沼反，下古暗反。</sub>幅<sub class="note-yuan">音福。</sub>裨<sub class="note-yuan">音卑。冕也。</sub>袜<sub class="note-yuan">望發反。</sub>㡊<sub class="note-yuan">苦洽反。</sub>琁<sub class="note-yuan">音旋。</sub>蟪<sub class="note-yuan">音惠。</sub>勁悍<sub class="note-yuan">音翰。</sub>縰<sub class="note-yuan">山綺反。纚同。</sub>咋<sub class="note-yuan">鉏陌反，又壯伯反。</sub>纚<sub class="note-yuan">所綺反。</sub>鷸<sub class="note-yuan">音聿。</sub>幡<sub class="note-yuan">孚袁反。</sub>噲<sub class="note-yuan">音快。</sub>鶡<sub class="note-yuan">音曷。</sub>𤪌<sub class="note-yuan">音其。</sub>邸<sub class="note-yuan">音底。</sub>尖韎<sub class="note-yuan">上子廉反，下音昩。</sub>哻<sub class="note-yuan">況羽反。</sub>頟<sub class="note-yuan">武彪反。</sub>翡<sub class="note-yuan">扶沸反。</sub>于窴<sub class="note-yuan">音田，又音殿。</sub>婕妤<sub class="note-yuan">接、予二音。</sub>瓄<sub class="note-yuan">音獨。</sub>䥖<sub class="note-yuan">音奠。</sub>緄<sub class="note-yuan">古本反。</sub>幗<sub class="note-yuan">古獲反。</sub>

罩<sub class="note-yuan">都孝反。</sub>耜<sub class="note-yuan">音似。</sub>鴟夷<sub class="note-yuan">處脂反。</sub>鳸<sub class="note-yuan">音戶。</sub>廥<sub class="note-yuan">古兌反。</sub>琅玕<sub class="note-yuan">郎、干二音。</sub>梢<sub class="note-yuan">所交反。蒲梢，良馬也。</sub>裸<sub class="note-yuan">郎果反。</sub>赧<sub class="note-yuan">女版反。</sub>貸<sub class="note-yuan">音忒。</sub>菁茅<sub class="note-yuan">音精。</sub>孑<sub class="note-yuan">居力反。</sub>廡<sub class="note-yuan">音武。</sub>帑<sub class="note-yuan">他朗反。</sub>傕<sub class="note-yuan">音角。</sub>氾<sub class="note-yuan">音泛。</sub>啖<sub class="note-yuan">徒敢反。</sub>侯汶<sub class="note-yuan">音問。</sub>濺<sub class="note-yuan">子賤反。</sub>糇糧<sub class="note-yuan">侯、良二音。</sub>輸<sub class="note-yuan">音戍。</sub>稆<sub class="note-yuan">音呂。</sub>椹<sub class="note-yuan">食稔反。</sub>沴<sub class="note-yuan">音麗。</sub>荷鍤<sub class="note-yuan">初洽反。</sub>䴵<sub class="note-yuan">與餅同。</sub>賧<sub class="note-yuan">士濫反。蠻夷以財贖罪。</sub>挻<sub class="note-yuan">式連反。</sub>覬<sub class="note-yuan">音冀。</sub>芍<sub class="note-yuan">七削反。</sub>堨<sub class="note-yuan">音遏。</sub>斐<sub class="note-yuan">妃尾反。</sub>耬犁<sub class="note-yuan">音婁。</sub>溉<sub class="note-yuan">古礙反。</sub>舃鹵<sub class="note-yuan">昔、魯二音。</sub>完<sub class="note-yuan">音桓。</sub>沘<sub class="note-yuan">音比。</sub>販<sub class="note-yuan">方願反。</sub>殿最<sub class="note-yuan">都見反。</sub>汙<sub class="note-yuan">音烏。</sub>磽塉<sub class="note-yuan">上口交反，下音籍。</sub>螺蜯<sub class="note-yuan">上祿和反，下白項反。</sub>填淤<sub class="note-yuan">音於。</sub>孳<sub class="note-yuan">音茲。</sub>葦<sub class="note-yuan">音偉。</sub>沮<sub class="note-yuan">子據反。</sub>完<sub class="note-yuan">音丸。</sub>瓫<sub class="note-yuan">與盆同。</sub>滀<sub class="note-yuan">丑六反。</sub>涸<sub class="note-yuan">音鶴。</sub>賨<sub class="note-yuan">在宗反。</sub>占<sub class="note-yuan">之贍反。</sub>鈒<sub class="note-yuan">色立反。</sub>熯<sub class="note-yuan">音漢。</sub>氾勝<sub class="note-yuan">音凡。</sub>𩦎<sub class="note-yuan">音斐。</sub>鼇<sub class="note-yuan">并列反。</sub>賈販<sub class="note-yuan">音古，下同。</sub>杼柚<sub class="note-yuan">佇、逐二音。</sub>盬<sub class="note-yuan">音古。</sub>礫<sub class="note-yuan">音歷。</sub>鍥<sub class="note-yuan">公節反，又音契，又口頡反。</sub>

## 志第十七卷<sub class="note-yuan">晉書二十七</sub>

迪<sub class="note-yuan">音狄。</sub>虙生<sub class="note-yuan">音伏。</sub>眭<sub class="note-yuan">息爲反。</sub>雰<sub class="note-yuan">音紛。</sub>勰<sub class="note-yuan">音協。</sub>熲<sub class="note-yuan">音炅。本作穎。[^21]</sub>鈴<sub class="note-yuan">音零。</sub>掛<sub class="note-yuan">卦買反，又音卦。</sub>埜<sub class="note-yuan">古野字。</sub>萐莆<sub class="note-yuan">上所甲反，下音甫。</sub>綝<sub class="note-yuan">丑林反。</sub>珧<sub class="note-yuan">音遙。</sub>紞<sub class="note-yuan">都敢反。</sub>屐<sub class="note-yuan">其逆反。</sub>柝<sub class="note-yuan">音託。</sub>眊<sub class="note-yuan">莫報反。</sub>姏<sub class="note-yuan">武酣反，老女稱。</sub>隼<sub class="note-yuan">思尹反。</sub>恣睢<sub class="note-yuan">許悸反。</sub>茶陵<sub class="note-yuan">丈加反。</sub>昭穆<sub class="note-yuan">音韶。</sub>東莞<sub class="note-yuan">音官。</sub>冏<sub class="note-yuan">舉永反。</sub>航<sub class="note-yuan">胡郎反。</sub>艘<sub class="note-yuan">蘇曹反。</sub>胔<sub class="note-yuan">在智反。</sub>燠<sub class="note-yuan">於六反。</sub>霿<sub class="note-yuan">莫弄反。天氣下地不應。又音務。</sub>𣄸<sub class="note-yuan">[^22]音禍，與禍同。</sub>豸<sub class="note-yuan">池爾反。</sub>𡩻<sub class="note-yuan">音姑。</sub>帢<sub class="note-yuan">苦洽反。</sub>縹<sub class="note-yuan">敷沼反。</sub>褻<sub class="note-yuan">音薛。</sub>劘<sub class="note-yuan">音摩。</sub>䙅<sub class="note-yuan">一霄反。</sub>貊<sub class="note-yuan">音陌。</sub>炙<sub class="note-yuan">之夜反。</sub>絈<sub class="note-yuan">與帕同。莫格反，方言：帕頭，[^23]幧頭也。南楚江淮之間曰帕頭，自關以西秦晉之郊曰絡頭。字書：帕，頸巾者也。幧，且消反。</sub>毳<sub class="note-yuan">此芮反。</sub>擷<sub class="note-yuan">胡結反。</sub>鐓<sub class="note-yuan">徒猥反。</sub>屩<sub class="note-yuan">音腳。</sub>繐衰<sub class="note-yuan">音崔。</sub>帩<sub class="note-yuan">士遙反。</sub>楄<sub class="note-yuan">扶然反。</sub>髲<sub class="note-yuan">皮義反。</sub>殮<sub class="note-yuan">力驗反。</sub>菰<sub class="note-yuan">音孤。</sub>葆<sub class="note-yuan">音保。</sub>轜<sub class="note-yuan">音而。</sub>玘<sub class="note-yuan">音起。</sub>媼<sub class="note-yuan">烏老反。</sub>䖟<sub class="note-yuan">武庚反。</sub>

詆<sub class="note-yuan">都禮反。</sub>漳<sub class="note-yuan">諸良反。</sub>枹罕<sub class="note-yuan">上音扶，又甫于反。下音漢。</sub>𩇕<sub class="note-yuan">與靜同，本作靚。</sub>殪<sub class="note-yuan">於計反。</sub>趺<sub class="note-yuan">甫于反。</sub>釘釘<sub class="note-yuan">上音丁，下丁定反。</sub>猳<sub class="note-yuan">音加。牡豕也。</sub>豚<sub class="note-yuan">徒渾反。</sub>酗<sub class="note-yuan">香句反。</sub>區霿<sub class="note-yuan">上烏豆反，下亡豆反。</sub>蹉<sub class="note-yuan">七何反。</sub>幃<sub class="note-yuan">音韋。</sub>䡟輗<sub class="note-yuan">上匹計反，下五計反。</sub>剎<sub class="note-yuan">初轄反。</sub>絀<sub class="note-yuan">音黜。</sub>彭蜞<sub class="note-yuan">音其。</sub>駛<sub class="note-yuan">所吏反。</sub>秭<sub class="note-yuan">將几反。</sub>渦<sub class="note-yuan">音戈。</sub>犍<sub class="note-yuan">其焉反。</sub>呴<sub class="note-yuan">呼后反。</sub>胥<sub class="note-yuan">息魚反。</sub>朱提<sub class="note-yuan">殊、匙二音。</sub>滹沲<sub class="note-yuan">上火乎反，下音陁。</sub>浩亹<sub class="note-yuan">上古合反，下音門。</sub>亡位<sub class="note-yuan">音無，下同。</sub>貿<sub class="note-yuan">莫候反。春秋曰王師敗績於貿戎。[^24]</sub>嘻<sub class="note-yuan">許其反。</sub>漦<sub class="note-yuan">涘淄反。</sub>蟠<sub class="note-yuan">音盤。</sub>橑<sub class="note-yuan">音老。</sub>線<sub class="note-yuan">仙箭反。</sub>黿<sub class="note-yuan">愚袁反。</sub>蹠<sub class="note-yuan">之石反。</sub>濞<sub class="note-yuan">匹備反。</sub>暨<sub class="note-yuan">居未反。</sub>柘<sub class="note-yuan">之夜反。</sub>髭鬚<sub class="note-yuan">上即移反，下相俞反。</sub>蔚<sub class="note-yuan">音尉。</sub>

## 志第二十卷<sub class="note-yuan">晉書三十</sub>

痡<sub class="note-yuan">普胡反。</sub>犴<sub class="note-yuan">音岸。</sub>陬<sub class="note-yuan">子侯反。</sub>惛<sub class="note-yuan">音昏。</sub>扑<sub class="note-yuan">普木反。本一作⿱艹扑。</sub>臏<sub class="note-yuan">毗忍反。</sub>臯<sub class="note-yuan">音高。</sub>肺<sub class="note-yuan">芳廢反。</sub>鎔<sub class="note-yuan">音容。</sub>老旄<sub class="note-yuan">莫報反。</sub>蠢<sub class="note-yuan">丑江反，又丑用反。</sub>縶<sub class="note-yuan">張立反。</sub>宄<sub class="note-yuan">音軌。</sub>狙<sub class="note-yuan">七余反。</sub>殛<sub class="note-yuan">紀力反。</sub>榜<sub class="note-yuan">音彭。</sub>箠<sub class="note-yuan">之累反。</sub>鉆鑽<sub class="note-yuan">上巨淹反，下了亂反。所以穿也。</sub>讞<sub class="note-yuan">魚列反。</sub>耐<sub class="note-yuan">乃代反。</sub>應劭<sub class="note-yuan">音膺。</sub>菅蒯<sub class="note-yuan">上音姦。下音怪，又苦壞反。</sub>彧<sub class="note-yuan">於六反。</sub>痝<sub class="note-yuan">莫江反。</sub>鬻拳<sub class="note-yuan">育、權二音。</sub>鋸<sub class="note-yuan">音據。</sub>釱<sub class="note-yuan">音第，又音大。</sub>趾<sub class="note-yuan">音止。</sub>械<sub class="note-yuan">胡芥反。</sub>撾<sub class="note-yuan">陟瓜反。</sub>悝<sub class="note-yuan">音恢。</sub>狡<sub class="note-yuan">古巧反。</sub>糅<sub class="note-yuan">人又反，又女救反。</sub>猲<sub class="note-yuan">許葛反。相恐也。又起法反。</sub>謾<sub class="note-yuan">武安反。</sub>呵<sub class="note-yuan">呼何反。</sub>郵<sub class="note-yuan">音尤。</sub>烽燧<sub class="note-yuan">峯、遂二音。</sub>髠<sub class="note-yuan">苦昆反。</sub>梟菹<sub class="note-yuan">側疎反。</sub>歐<sub class="note-yuan">一口反。</sub>毋丘<sub class="note-yuan">音無。</sub>匄<sub class="note-yuan">音蓋。</sub>戕<sub class="note-yuan">疾良反。</sub>捍<sub class="note-yuan">音汗。</sub>銖<sub class="note-yuan">音殊。</sub>珽<sub class="note-yuan">他鼎反。</sub>蹴<sub class="note-yuan">取育反。</sub>頠<sub class="note-yuan">魚毀反。</sub>售<sub class="note-yuan">音授。</sub>概<sub class="note-yuan">古礙反。</sub>圮<sub class="note-yuan">符鄙反。</sub>嶷<sub class="note-yuan">魚力反。</sub>惇<sub class="note-yuan">音敦。</sub>

## 晉書音義卷之中<sub class="note-yuan">傳上</sub><sub class="note-yuan">起第三十一盡七十</sub>

## 列傳第一卷<sub class="note-yuan">晉書三十一</sub>

伉儷<sub class="note-yuan">抗、麗二音。</sub>敻<sub class="note-yuan">虛政反。</sub>嚳<sub class="note-yuan">苦沃反。</sub>朓<sub class="note-yuan">漢書音吐彫反，[^25]又吐鳥反。</sub>龍漦<sub class="note-yuan">史記音俟其反，字林丑之反。</sub>挻<sub class="note-yuan">式連反。</sub>枌<sub class="note-yuan">音汾。</sub>甄<sub class="note-yuan">音真。</sub>孌<sub class="note-yuan">力兗反。</sub>詖<sub class="note-yuan">音簡。</sub>語<sub class="note-yuan">魚據反。</sub>蓀<sub class="note-yuan">音孫。</sub>瘁<sub class="note-yuan">疾醉反。</sub>窀穸<sub class="note-yuan">屯、夕二音。</sub>王假<sub class="note-yuan">烏感反。</sub>躊躇<sub class="note-yuan">儔、除二音。</sub>翣<sub class="note-yuan">所甲反。</sub>嫕<sub class="note-yuan">於計反。</sub>悝<sub class="note-yuan">苦回反。</sub>要募<sub class="note-yuan">音劉。</sub>歔欷<sub class="note-yuan">上音虛，下許既反。</sub>號咷<sub class="note-yuan">豪、桃二音。</sub>漣洏<sub class="note-yuan">連、而二音。</sub>蔟<sub class="note-yuan">倉谷反。</sub>繭<sub class="note-yuan">音梅。</sub>峕<sub class="note-yuan">古時字。</sub>踧踖<sub class="note-yuan">上子六反，下音迹。</sub>珧<sub class="note-yuan">音姚。</sub>廝<sub class="note-yuan">音斯。</sub>嫗<sub class="note-yuan">紆遇反。</sub>簏<sub class="note-yuan">音鹿。</sub>寺人<sub class="note-yuan">時志反。</sub>暾<sub class="note-yuan">他昆反。</sub>祊<sub class="note-yuan">甫彭反。</sub>

class="note-yuan">呂覽云：覆以玉筐，少選而發視之。高誘云：少選，須臾之頃也。珠叢少音尸紹反。選音先兗反，又音雪絹反。</sub>幢<sub class="note-yuan">宅江反。</sub>郫<sub class="note-yuan">音皮。</sub>共驩<sub class="note-yuan">上音恭。</sub>

笞<sub class="note-yuan">丑支反。</sub>骼<sub class="note-yuan">音格。</sub>胔<sub class="note-yuan">在智反。</sub>幢<sub class="note-yuan">宅江反。</sub>敦煌<sub class="note-yuan">屯、皇二音。</sub>洮<sub class="note-yuan">音盤。</sub>駘<sub class="note-yuan">徒亥反。</sub>噫<sub class="note-yuan">於其反。</sub>嘔喁<sub class="note-yuan">上於武反，下虞矩反。</sub>函<sub class="note-yuan">音咸。</sub>瑣<sub class="note-yuan">音毗。</sub>壝<sub class="note-yuan">以季反。周禮大司徒掌社稷之壝。[^34]</sub>枑<sub class="note-yuan">音互。行馬也。</sub>黕<sub class="note-yuan">丁感反。</sub>阼<sub class="note-yuan">在護反。</sub>蔥犗<sub class="note-yuan">古邁反。</sub>縹軛<sub class="note-yuan">上匹妙反，下烏革反。</sub>紺<sub class="note-yuan">古憾反。</sub>耜<sub class="note-yuan">鄭玄禮記注：耜，耒之金也。音似。</sub>幰<sub class="note-yuan">釋名云：車幔也，所以御熱也。[^35]虛偃反。</sub>魚麗<sub class="note-yuan">呂知反。</sub>穜稑<sub class="note-yuan">鄭玄周禮注云：先種後熟謂之穜，後種先熟謂之稑。上直龍反，下力竹反。</sub>挈壺<sub class="note-yuan">苦結反。</sub>錚鎗<sub class="note-yuan">上又莖反，下又莊反。</sub>綃<sub class="note-yuan">音消。</sub>綷䌨<sub class="note-yuan">上七碎反，下七大反。</sub>沛艾<sub class="note-yuan">上普賴反，下五賴反。</sub>縞<sub class="note-yuan">古老反。</sub>鈒<sub class="note-yuan">工迥反。</sub>縻<sub class="note-yuan">牛轡也。靡宜反。</sub>遝<sub class="note-yuan">徒合反。</sub>頒斌<sub class="note-yuan">上版蠻反，下甫巾反。</sub>髫<sub class="note-yuan">大聊反。</sub>掎<sub class="note-yuan">居靡反。</sub>襼<sub class="note-yuan">與袂同。當作褹，複襦也。音藝。李善注文選亦作襼，即袂字。今依諸本及音義。</sub>祧<sub class="note-yuan">他堯反。</sub>諏<sub class="note-yuan">足俱反。</sub>簠簋<sub 蟹<sub class="note-yuan">下買反。</sub>斲喪<sub class="note-yuan">息浪反。</sub>欒郤<sub class="note-yuan">綺戟反。</sub>認<sub class="note-yuan">而震反。</sub>旂<sub class="note-yuan">渠希反。</sub>趫<sub class="note-yuan">去遙反。</sub>悍<sub class="note-yuan">音汗。</sub>慴<sub class="note-yuan">之涉反。</sub>鞞<sub class="note-yuan">步迷反。</sub>繆<sub class="note-yuan">音謬。</sub>郝<sub class="note-yuan">呼各反。</sub>坫<sub class="note-yuan">都念反。</sub>睢<sub class="note-yuan">七余反。</sub>躓<sub class="note-yuan">音致。</sub>奭<sub class="note-yuan">音適。</sub>羆<sub class="note-yuan">彼爲反。</sub>帴<sub class="note-yuan">側前反。</sub>薈<sub class="note-yuan">烏外反。</sub>塢<sub class="note-yuan">烏古反。</sub>𣈶<sub class="note-yuan">況晚反，又古鄧反。</sub>綝<sub class="note-yuan">丑林反。</sub>靖<sub class="note-yuan">疾郢反。</sub>氾衷<sub class="note-yuan">凡、忠二音。</sub>甝<sub class="note-yuan">胡甘反。</sub>紾<sub class="note-yuan">之忍反。</sub>虫蛇<sub class="note-yuan">虛鬼反。</sub>虬蟉<sub class="note-yuan">渠糾反。</sub>阿那<sub class="note-yuan">上烏可反，下乃可反。</sub>欻<sub class="note-yuan">許物反。</sub>肸<sub class="note-yuan">許訖反。</sub>窊<sub class="note-yuan">烏瓜反。</sub>踞<sub class="note-yuan">音據。</sub>差<sub class="note-yuan">初宜反。</sub>窈嬈<sub class="note-yuan">上烏皎反，下奴鳥反。</sub>苫<sub class="note-yuan">失廉反。</sub>猗<sub class="note-yuan">音倚。</sub>螭<sub class="note-yuan">丑知反。</sub>鼺<sub class="note-yuan">力尾反。</sub>俶儻<sub class="note-yuan">他朗反，又他浪反。</sub>析<sub class="note-yuan">先擊反。</sub>磥<sub class="note-yuan">虛罪反。</sub>腕<sub class="note-yuan">烏段反。</sub>鯁綣<sub class="note-yuan">去院反。</sub>芟<sub class="note-yuan">所銜反。</sub>蝮<sub class="note-yuan">芳伏反。</sub>螫<sub class="note-yuan">施尺反。</sub>稆<sub class="note-yuan">音呂。</sub>竺<sub class="note-yuan">陟六反。</sub>獷<sub class="note-yuan">古猛反。</sub>

浚<sub class="note-yuan">私閏反。</sub>俘馘<sub class="note-yuan">古獲反。</sub>惲<sub class="note-yuan">於粉反。</sub>狷<sub class="note-yuan">吉掾反。</sub>詆<sub class="note-yuan">都禮反。</sub>碌<sub class="note-yuan">盧谷反。</sub>衰范<sub class="note-yuan">初危反。</sub>眕<sub class="note-yuan">之忍反。</sub>崤<sub class="note-yuan">胡茅反。</sub>險澀<sub class="note-yuan">所立反。</sub>漕<sub class="note-yuan">在到反。</sub>晞<sub class="note-yuan">音希。</sub>紓<sub class="note-yuan">音舒。</sub>虓<sub class="note-yuan">許交反。</sub>柵<sub class="note-yuan">楚革反。</sub>壘<sub class="note-yuan">力軌反。</sub>援枹<sub class="note-yuan">芳于反，本與桴同。</sub>羯<sub class="note-yuan">居謁反。</sub>懣<sub class="note-yuan">亡本反。</sub>劫<sub class="note-yuan">居怯反。</sub>瑁<sub class="note-yuan">莫佩反。</sub>盾<sub class="note-yuan">徒損反。</sub>軼<sub class="note-yuan">夷質反。</sub>悝<sub class="note-yuan">苦回反。</sub>崎嶇<sub class="note-yuan">上去奇反，下音區。</sub>袪<sub class="note-yuan">去魚反。</sub>鞮<sub class="note-yuan">當奚反。</sub>簏<sub class="note-yuan">音祿。</sub>

## 列傳第三十二卷<sub class="note-yuan">晉書六十二</sub>

荂<sub class="note-yuan">音孚。</sub>帀<sub class="note-yuan">子荅反。</sub>楯<sub class="note-yuan">食尹反。</sub>鞮<sub class="note-yuan">當奚反。[^38]</sub>鞬<sub class="note-yuan">舉軒反。</sub>耨<sub class="note-yuan">奴豆反。</sub>勞徠<sub class="note-yuan">上盧到反，下音賴。</sub>猗㐌<sub class="note-yuan">上於離反，下弋支反。</sub>痍<sub class="note-yuan">音夷。</sub>拓<sub class="note-yuan">音託。</sub>覆餗<sub class="note-yuan">音速。</sub>翅<sub class="note-yuan">施智反。</sub>蟻<sub class="note-yuan">魚豈反。</sub>殪<sub class="note-yuan">於計反。</sub>磾<sub class="note-yuan">堂奚反。</sub>喢<sub class="note-yuan">所洽反。</sub>邏騎<sub class="note-yuan">盧箇反。遊兵也。</sub>慷愾<sub class="note-yuan">[^39]慨、慨二音。</sub>輈<sub class="note-yuan">張留反。</sub>攄<sub class="note-yuan">敕居反。</sub>縊<sub class="note-yuan">於賜反。</sub>擾攘<sub class="note-yuan">如兩反。</sub>頓仆<sub class="note-yuan">音赴。</sub>跋<sub class="note-yuan">蒲撥反。</sub>櫜<sub class="note-yuan">音高。</sub>驎<sub class="note-yuan">力珍反。</sub>踦𨄅<sub class="note-yuan">上去奇反，下音區。</sub>藜<sub class="note-yuan">落奚反。</sub>猶膩<sub class="note-yuan">女利反。</sub>械<sub class="note-yuan">胡芥反。</sub>殮<sub class="note-yuan">力驗反。</sub>疽<sub class="note-yuan">七余反。</sub>鴦<sub class="note-yuan">於良反，又烏郎反。</sub>遒<sub class="note-yuan">子由反。</sub>賙<sub class="note-yuan">音周。</sub>蹴<sub class="note-yuan">取育反。</sub>剽<sub class="note-yuan">匹笑反。</sub>鎧<sub class="note-yuan">苦愛反。</sub>楫<sub class="note-yuan">紫葉反。</sub>譎<sub class="note-yuan">古穴反。</sub>擔<sub class="note-yuan">都濫反。</sub>樵<sub class="note-yuan">昨焦反。</sub>醊<sub class="note-yuan">音綴。</sub>妖<sub class="note-yuan">於喬反。</sub>羖<sub class="note-yuan">音古。</sub>喈<sub class="note-yuan">音皆。</sub>蹠<sub class="note-yuan">之石反。</sub>錐<sub class="note-yuan">職追反。</sub>捶<sub class="note-yuan">之累反。</sub>佻巧<sub class="note-yuan">吐彫反。</sub>跅弛<sub class="note-yuan">上音託，下式是反。</sub>圄<sub class="note-yuan">音語。</sub>獯<sub class="note-yuan">音勳。</sub>

亟<sub class="note-yuan">去吏反。</sub>掠<sub class="note-yuan">力勺反。</sub>齔<sub class="note-yuan">初謹反。</sub>塢<sub class="note-yuan">烏古反。</sub>堨<sub class="note-yuan">烏葛反。</sub>薈<sub class="note-yuan">烏外反。</sub>軺<sub class="note-yuan">音遙。</sub>堮<sub class="note-yuan">五各反。</sub>猗<sub class="note-yuan">於離反。</sub>麈<sub class="note-yuan">音主。</sub>峴<sub class="note-yuan">胡典反。</sub>槊<sub class="note-yuan">所角反。</sub>洎<sub class="note-yuan">其器反。</sub>酖<sub class="note-yuan">[^40]字當作鴆。直任反。</sub>繹幕<sub class="note-yuan">顏音弋尺反。</sub>襁<sub class="note-yuan">居兩反。</sub>該<sub class="note-yuan">古哀反。</sub>倮<sub class="note-yuan">郎果反。</sub>肫<sub class="note-yuan">徒昆反。</sub>孔煒<sub class="note-yuan">于鬼反。</sub>呴<sub class="note-yuan">呼后反。虓呴也。</sub>眦睚<sub class="note-yuan">上士懈反，下五懈反。</sub>戾<sub class="note-yuan">協韵慮結反。</sub>

覘<sub class="note-yuan">敕艷反。</sub>愀<sub class="note-yuan">茲糾反，又子了反。</sub>泮<sub class="note-yuan">普半反。</sub>龕<sub class="note-yuan">音堪。</sub>隗<sub class="note-yuan">五罪反。</sub>帑<sub class="note-yuan">他朗反。金帛舍也。</sub>綀<sub class="note-yuan">色魚反。</sub>賵<sub class="note-yuan">撫諷反。</sub>襚<sub class="note-yuan">音遂。</sub>轀輬<sub class="note-yuan">溫、涼二音。</sub>纛<sub class="note-yuan">音導。</sub>檮杌<sub class="note-yuan">上徒刀反，下五忽反。</sub>圮<sub class="note-yuan">符鄙反。</sub>襞<sub class="note-yuan">字林曰：襞，卷衣也。音必益反。</sub>嘏<sub class="note-yuan">古雅反。</sub>曬<sub class="note-yuan">所買反。</sub>如椽<sub class="note-yuan">直緣反。</sub>曇<sub class="note-yuan">徒含反。</sub>墮<sub class="note-yuan">徒臥反。</sub>饘<sub class="note-yuan">諸然反。</sub>飴<sub class="note-yuan">音嗣。</sub>廞<sub class="note-yuan">許金反。</sub>隼<sub class="note-yuan">思尹反。</sub>櫛<sub class="note-yuan">阻瑟反。</sub>隤<sub class="note-yuan">杜回反。</sub>贙<sub class="note-yuan">胡犬反。</sub>

渟<sub class="note-yuan">音亭。</sub>窅<sub class="note-yuan">烏了反。</sub>窐<sub class="note-yuan">烏華反。</sub>悛<sub class="note-yuan">此緣反。</sub>斁<sub class="note-yuan">音妬。</sub>汩<sub class="note-yuan">音骨。</sub>𱈟<sub class="note-yuan">與斥同。</sub>顰顣<sub class="note-yuan">上脾賓反，下子六反。</sub>梠<sub class="note-yuan">音呂。</sub>螊𧑅<sub class="note-yuan">上乎緘反。字林曰：螊，海蟲，長一寸，可食。下音越。</sub>帀<sub class="note-yuan">子荅反。</sub>撞<sub class="note-yuan">宅江反。</sub>挑柈<sub class="note-yuan">音盤。</sub>螮蝀<sub class="note-yuan">上都計反，下多動反。</sub>忼愾<sub class="note-yuan">音慷慨。</sub>踧踖<sub class="note-yuan">上子六反，下資昔反。</sub>柂<sub class="note-yuan">徒可反。</sub>櫓<sub class="note-yuan">音魯。</sub>鯔鰞<sub class="note-yuan">音淄烏。</sub>鯆<sub class="note-yuan">音鋪。</sub>䱐<sub class="note-yuan">音浮。埤蒼：䱐，䱡魚也。說文：䱡魚出樂浪番國，一名江豚，多膏少肉。一曰出江，有兩乳。[^59]䱡音居六反。</sub>鷁<sub class="note-yuan">五歷反。</sub>梢<sub class="note-yuan">所交反。</sub>跳<sub class="note-yuan">徒聊反。</sub>喉囀<sub class="note-yuan">知戀反。</sub>嗽<sub class="note-yuan">蘇豆反。</sub>咤<sub class="note-yuan">陟嫁反。</sub>讙<sub class="note-yuan">況袁反。</sub>葭<sub class="note-yuan">音加。</sub>袿<sub class="note-yuan">音圭。</sub>襡<sub class="note-yuan">音蜀。字林曰：袿，婦人上衣也。襡，連要衣也。</sub>認<sub class="note-yuan">而晉反。</sub>涫<sub class="note-yuan">音官。</sub>媟<sub class="note-yuan">私列反。</sub>頵<sub class="note-yuan">居筠反，又於倫反。</sub>哿<sub class="note-yuan">古我反。</sub>荀崧<sub class="note-yuan">音嵩。</sub>貿<sub class="note-yuan">莫候反。</sub>菴<sub class="note-yuan">烏含反。</sub>袴褶<sub class="note-yuan">神執反。</sub>踑踞<sub class="note-yuan">居御反。</sub>瞪眸<sub class="note-yuan">直陵、直耕二反。</sub>梁踦<sub class="note-yuan">魚綺、其綺二反。</sub>墊<sub class="note-yuan">都念反。</sub>鮞<sub class="note-yuan">音而。</sub>饋<sub class="note-yuan">求位反。</sub>歠<sub class="note-yuan">昌雪反。</sub>驎<sub class="note-yuan">力珍反。</sub>囷<sub class="note-yuan">去倫反。</sub>纁<sub class="note-yuan">音勳。</sub>餼<sub class="note-yuan">許既反。</sub>羯<sub class="note-yuan">居謁反。</sub>倮<sub class="note-yuan">郎果反。</sub>鐃<sub class="note-yuan">女交反。</sub>嘏<sub class="note-yuan">古雅反。</sub>銖<sub class="note-yuan">音殊。</sub>瞿硎<sub class="note-yuan">客庚反。</sub>汁溲<sub class="note-yuan">疎有反。</sub>縶<sub class="note-yuan">陟立反。</sub>秫<sub class="note-yuan">音述。</sub>秔<sub class="note-yuan">古行反。</sub>拳拳<sub class="note-yuan">巨員反。</sub>芸秄<sub class="note-yuan">音云子。叶韵音茲。</sub>籃輿<sub class="note-yuan">盧甘反。</sub>颯<sub class="note-yuan">蘇合反。</sub>敻<sub class="note-yuan">虛政反。</sub>泌<sub class="note-yuan">毗必反。</sub>

讖<sub class="note-yuan">初譖反。</sub>眕<sub class="note-yuan">之忍反。</sub>闓<sub class="note-yuan">音開。</sub>蠡<sub class="note-yuan">音禮。</sub>禺<sub class="note-yuan">音隅。</sub>胃昴<sub class="note-yuan">音謂卯。</sub>炬<sub class="note-yuan">音巨。</sub>偵邏<sub class="note-yuan">丑鄭、盧箇二反。</sub>賽<sub class="note-yuan">先代反。</sub>魅<sub class="note-yuan">美祕反。</sub>鼉<sub class="note-yuan">徒何反。</sub>脹<sub class="note-yuan">陟亮反。</sub>欻<sub class="note-yuan">許物反。</sub>大鸜<sub class="note-yuan">共俱反。</sub>齧<sub class="note-yuan">五結反。</sub>嘷<sub class="note-yuan">胡刀反。</sub>拉<sub class="note-yuan">盧合反。</sub>搥拍<sub class="note-yuan">上直追反，下普伯反。</sub>咋<sub class="note-yuan">鉏陌反。</sub>浚<sub class="note-yuan">私閏反。</sub>羆<sub class="note-yuan">彼爲反。</sub>駮<sub class="note-yuan">北角反。</sub>隗炤<sub class="note-yuan">音照。</sub>蓍<sub class="note-yuan">式脂反。</sub>珝<sub class="note-yuan">況羽反。</sub>靚<sub class="note-yuan">疾政反。</sub>捍<sub class="note-yuan">音翰。</sub>鴨<sub class="note-yuan">烏押反。</sub>蚊<sub class="note-yuan">音文。</sub>痿痹<sub class="note-yuan">上人垂反，下必至反。</sub>訶叱<sub class="note-yuan">上呼何反，下齒日反。</sub>絮<sub class="note-yuan">息據反。</sub>鉢<sub class="note-yuan">博末反。</sub>枋<sub class="note-yuan">府良反。</sub>劬<sub class="note-yuan">其居反。</sub>肘<sub class="note-yuan">陟柳反。</sub>鷗<sub class="note-yuan">烏侯反。</sub>懺<sub class="note-yuan">初鑒反。</sub>黿<sub class="note-yuan">音元。</sub>噀<sub class="note-yuan">蘇困反。</sub>菅<sub class="note-yuan">古顏反。</sub>蛻<sub class="note-yuan">託臥反。</sub>紞<sub class="note-yuan">都敢反。</sub>泮<sub class="note-yuan">普半反。</sub>啖<sub class="note-yuan">徒敢反。</sub>濛汜<sub class="note-yuan">蒙、似二音。</sub>島<sub class="note-yuan">與嶋同。</sub>滑稽<sub class="note-yuan">音骨雞。</sub>叉<sub class="note-yuan">初牙反。</sub>紕<sub class="note-yuan">匹夷反。</sub>曇<sub class="note-yuan">徒含反。</sub>睫<sub class="note-yuan">紫葉反。</sub>

娀<sub class="note-yuan">息融反。</sub>㜪<sub class="note-yuan">所臻反。</sub>姙<sub class="note-yuan">如林反。</sub>韡<sub class="note-yuan">韋鬼反。</sub>翅<sub class="note-yuan">施智反。</sub>郝<sub class="note-yuan">好各反。</sub>袤<sub class="note-yuan">莫候反。</sub>浣<sub class="note-yuan">胡管反。</sub>瘞<sub class="note-yuan">於例反。</sub>坩鮓<sub class="note-yuan">苦甘反。</sub>剉<sub class="note-yuan">麄臥反。</sub>晞<sub class="note-yuan">音希。</sub>藐<sub class="note-yuan">妙小反。</sub>弢<sub class="note-yuan">吐高反。</sub>覘<sub class="note-yuan">敕艷反。</sub>碌<sub class="note-yuan">音祿。</sub>崧<sub class="note-yuan">音嵩。</sub>葩<sub class="note-yuan">普巴反。</sub>剔緜<sub class="note-yuan">他歷反。</sub>登橙<sub class="note-yuan">等鄧反。</sub>䳨<sub class="note-yuan">音皇。</sub>壼<sub class="note-yuan">苦本反。</sub>彎<sub class="note-yuan">烏關反。</sub>娩<sub class="note-yuan">晚、免二音。</sub>

夫餘<sub class="note-yuan">音扶。</sub>罽<sub class="note-yuan">居厲反。</sub>貂豽<sub class="note-yuan">女滑反。</sub>穢王<sub class="note-yuan">於肺、乙劣二反。</sub>沃沮<sub class="note-yuan">子余反。</sub>紒<sub class="note-yuan">亦髻字。音計。</sub>蹻<sub class="note-yuan">其虐、居灼二音。</sub>讙<sub class="note-yuan">音喧。</sub>楯<sub class="note-yuan">食允反。</sub>扁<sub class="note-yuan">音褊。</sub>襜<sub class="note-yuan">處詹反。</sub>砮<sub class="note-yuan">奴古反。</sub>楛<sub class="note-yuan">音戶。</sub>傉雞<sub class="note-yuan">內沃反。</sub>倭<sub class="note-yuan">烏禾反。</sub>孥<sub class="note-yuan">音奴。</sub>釤<sub class="note-yuan">所監反。</sub>羃䍦<sub class="note-yuan">音覓離。</sub>氂<sub class="note-yuan">理之反。</sub>酋<sub class="note-yuan">字秋反。</sub>拔<sub class="note-yuan">蒲撥反。</sub>葉<sub class="note-yuan">音攝。</sub>恍惚<sub class="note-yuan">上呼晃反，下呼骨反。</sub>涸<sub class="note-yuan">下各反。</sub>愞<sub class="note-yuan">乃亂反。</sub>汗<sub class="note-yuan">音寒。</sub>漒<sub class="note-yuan">音強。</sub>蹄<sub class="note-yuan">音題。詩云白蹢，音的。</sub>襦<sub class="note-yuan">日朱反。</sub>獪<sub class="note-yuan">古邁反。</sub>賁崙<sub class="note-yuan">音奔。</sub>月氏<sub class="note-yuan">音支。</sub>賈<sub class="note-yuan">音古。</sub>銖<sub class="note-yuan">音殊。</sub>顥<sub class="note-yuan">音昊。</sub>薤<sub class="note-yuan">胡界反。</sub>犁鞬<sub class="note-yuan">上落奚反，下居言反。</sub>梲栭<sub class="note-yuan">音拙而。</sub>礎<sub class="note-yuan">音楚。</sub>合縫<sub class="note-yuan">下音俸。</sub>西卷<sub class="note-yuan">漢書音卷，又音權。</sub>鏟<sub class="note-yuan">初限反。</sub>估較<sub class="note-yuan">音古角。</sub>鉦<sub class="note-yuan">之成反。</sub>大灣<sub class="note-yuan">烏關反。</sub>舶<sub class="note-yuan">音白。</sub>獷<sub class="note-yuan">古猛反。</sub>萎莎<sub class="note-yuan">蘇和反。</sub>蛭<sub class="note-yuan">之日反。</sub>蠡<sub class="note-yuan">音螺。</sub>綦毋<sub class="note-yuan">音無。</sub>俔<sub class="note-yuan">苦見反。</sub>韝<sub class="note-yuan">古候反。</sub>

敺<sub class="note-yuan">烏口反。</sub>磾<sub class="note-yuan">音低。</sub>坫<sub class="note-yuan">都念反。</sub>閫<sub class="note-yuan">苦本反。</sub>陳頒<sub class="note-yuan">布還反。</sub>帑<sub class="note-yuan">他朗反。</sub>嚭<sub class="note-yuan">匹鄙反。</sub>犒<sub class="note-yuan">苦到反。</sub>剽掠<sub class="note-yuan">上匹笑反，下力讓反。</sub>鬻拳<sub class="note-yuan">音育權。</sub>莚<sub class="note-yuan">音延。</sub>煽<sub class="note-yuan">音扇。</sub>跽<sub class="note-yuan">暨儿反。</sub>桁<sub class="note-yuan">胡郎反。</sub>輗車<sub class="note-yuan">輗，礙車也。五奚反。</sub>枹<sub class="note-yuan">音俘。</sub>棱<sub class="note-yuan">落登反。</sub>蝟<sub class="note-yuan">云貴反。</sub>磔<sub class="note-yuan">陟格反。</sub>魚復<sub class="note-yuan">應劭音腹。</sub>笮橋<sub class="note-yuan">側陌反。</sub>葭萌<sub class="note-yuan">音加。</sub>昝<sub class="note-yuan">子感反。</sub>噂𠴲<sub class="note-yuan">上茲損反，下徒合反。</sub>淅<sub class="note-yuan">音錫。</sub>芟<sub class="note-yuan">音衫。</sub>潸<sub class="note-yuan">數扳反。</sub>褫<sub class="note-yuan">池爾反。</sub>牸<sub class="note-yuan">音字。</sub>鞸<sub class="note-yuan">補鼎反。</sub>䩬<sub class="note-yuan">方孔反。</sub>赭圻<sub class="note-yuan">上之野反，下渠希反。</sub>柈<sub class="note-yuan">音盤。</sub>涂<sub class="note-yuan">度都反。</sub>蚝<sub class="note-yuan">七吏反。</sub>慴<sub class="note-yuan">之涉反。</sub>祟<sub class="note-yuan">雖遂反。</sub>纛<sub class="note-yuan">徒到反。</sub>鄳人<sub class="note-yuan">蘇林音盲，字林芒耿反。</sub>睥睨<sub class="note-yuan">上匹詣反，下五計反。</sub>懟<sub class="note-yuan">直類反。</sub>浞<sub class="note-yuan">士捉反。</sub>𤡬<sub class="note-yuan">於計反。</sub>

冏<sub class="note-yuan">舉永反。</sub>瓢<sub class="note-yuan">符遙反。</sub>妳媼<sub class="note-yuan">上奴解反，下烏浩反。</sub>湓<sub class="note-yuan">蒲奔反。</sub>悍<sub class="note-yuan">胡幹反。</sub>虓<sub class="note-yuan">呼交反。</sub>跋扈<sub class="note-yuan">步撥反。</sub>蹙<sub class="note-yuan">子六反。</sub>綟<sub class="note-yuan">盧結反。</sub>梠<sub class="note-yuan">音呂。</sub>舸<sub class="note-yuan">古我反。</sub>彬<sub class="note-yuan">甫巾反。</sub>榜<sub class="note-yuan">博朗反。</sub>衽<sub class="note-yuan">如甚反。</sub>𨎪<sub class="note-yuan">音而。</sub>蒐<sub class="note-yuan">所愁反。</sub>讙<sub class="note-yuan">音喧。</sub>帔<sub class="note-yuan">彼義反。</sub>偵<sub class="note-yuan">丑鄭反。</sub>譟<sub class="note-yuan">蘇到反。</sub>喁<sub class="note-yuan">魚容反。</sub>譁<sub class="note-yuan">音花。</sub>衄<sub class="note-yuan">女六反。</sub>舫<sub class="note-yuan">府望反。</sub>磯<sub class="note-yuan">居希反。</sub>柞<sub class="note-yuan">在各反，又則洛反。</sub>溳<sub class="note-yuan">音云。</sub>酗醟<sub class="note-yuan">上香句反，下音詠。</sub>狙<sub class="note-yuan">七余反。</sub>幺麽<sub class="note-yuan">上一堯反，下亡可反。</sub>

惤<sub class="note-yuan">古賢反。本作怰。</sub>券<sub class="note-yuan">去願反。</sub>撍<sub class="note-yuan">子感反。</sub>傴<sub class="note-yuan">於武反。</sub>下儁<sub class="note-yuan">辭兗反。</sub>腐<sub class="note-yuan">扶雨反。</sub>絆<sub class="note-yuan">音半。</sub>涅陽<sub class="note-yuan">奴結反。</sub>莠<sub class="note-yuan">音酉。</sub>呵<sub class="note-yuan">呼何反。</sub>弢<sub class="note-yuan">吐高反。</sub>箋<sub class="note-yuan">則前反。</sub>崧<sub class="note-yuan">音嵩。</sub>臠<sub class="note-yuan">力兗反。</sub>眕<sub class="note-yuan">之忍反。</sub>眺<sub class="note-yuan">他鳥反。</sub>踧<sub class="note-yuan">丁六反。</sub>汶衡<sub class="note-yuan">亡運反。</sub>紐<sub class="note-yuan">女久反。</sub>歃<sub class="note-yuan">山洽反。</sub>鮪<sub class="note-yuan">榮美反。</sub>貰<sub class="note-yuan">舒制反，又時夜反。</sub>皖<sub class="note-yuan">戶版反，又胡官反。縣名，在廬江。</sub>盱眙<sub class="note-yuan">與之反。</sub>柵<sub class="note-yuan">惻戟反。</sub>攘<sub class="note-yuan">如兩反。</sub>裸<sub class="note-yuan">郎果反。</sub>羕<sub class="note-yuan">餘亮反。</sub>沮衄<sub class="note-yuan">上慈呂反，下女六反。</sub>怯懦<sub class="note-yuan">上去業反，下奴亂反。</sub>庱<sub class="note-yuan">丑升反。</sub>靫<sub class="note-yuan">音釵。</sub>勰<sub class="note-yuan">音叶。</sub>蛻<sub class="note-yuan">託臥反。</sub>尩<sub class="note-yuan">烏光反。</sub>傿<sub class="note-yuan">於建反。</sub>堙<sub class="note-yuan">音因。</sub>簏<sub class="note-yuan">音祿。</sub>贛<sub class="note-yuan">音感。</sub>柤浦<sub class="note-yuan">側加反。</sub>譎<sub class="note-yuan">古穴反。</sub>翌<sub class="note-yuan">音翼。</sub>盥<sub class="note-yuan">古纂反，又去聲。</sub>痡<sub class="note-yuan">普胡反。</sub>蜮<sub class="note-yuan">音或。</sub>踣<sub class="note-yuan">傍北反。</sub>

## 載記第一卷<sub class="note-yuan">晉書一百一</sub>

湩<sub class="note-yuan">乳汁也。多貢反，又竹用反。</sub>詖<sub class="note-yuan">彼義反。</sub>覘<sub class="note-yuan">敕廉、敕艷二反。</sub>盟津<sub class="note-yuan">音孟。</sub>劃<sub class="note-yuan">胡麥反。</sub>沮渠<sub class="note-yuan">子魚反。</sub>冒頓<sub class="note-yuan">莫北反。</sub>奧鞬<sub class="note-yuan">上於六反，下居言反。</sub>鬐<sub class="note-yuan">渠脂反</sub>。覡<sub class="note-yuan">胡狄反。</sub>邯鄲<sub class="note-yuan">寒、丹二音。</sub>齠齔<sub class="note-yuan">上音條，一本作髫。下初謹反。</sub>賻<sub class="note-yuan">音附。</sub>磾<sub class="note-yuan">音低</sub>珧<sub class="note-yuan">餘昭反。</sub>培塿<sub class="note-yuan">蒲口反，盧斗反。</sub>拉<sub class="note-yuan">盧合反。</sub>崎嶇<sub class="note-yuan">上去奇反，下音區。</sub>拓<sub class="note-yuan">音託。</sub>閹<sub class="note-yuan">音奄。宦官，又豎也，宮中閹閽閉門者。</sub>聶<sub class="note-yuan">尼輒反。</sub>泫氏<sub class="note-yuan">胡犬反。</sub>氐<sub class="note-yuan">當奚反。</sub>酋<sub class="note-yuan">字秋反。</sub>顥<sub class="note-yuan">音浩。</sub>

## 載記第二卷<sub class="note-yuan">晉書一百二</sub>

彎<sub class="note-yuan">烏關反。</sub>鹿蠡<sub class="note-yuan">韋昭音離。</sub>驁<sub class="note-yuan">五勞反。</sub>肺腑<sub class="note-yuan">音府。</sub>疋<sub class="note-yuan">與雅同。</sub>柘<sub class="note-yuan">之夜反。</sub>銀研<sub class="note-yuan">五見反。</sub>珝<sub class="note-yuan">況雨反。</sub>狼猛<sub class="note-yuan">漢書狼音浪。</sub>殿屎<sub class="note-yuan">詩云：人之方殿屎。爾雅云：殿屎，呻吟也。孫炎云：愁苦呻吟之聲。殿音丁見反，屎音香伊反。字林火迷反。</sub>狃<sub class="note-yuan">女久反。</sub>戇<sub class="note-yuan">丁降反。</sub>蹄涔<sub class="note-yuan">助簪反。</sub>悸<sub class="note-yuan">其季反。</sub>佻<sub class="note-yuan">吐彫反。</sub>袤<sub class="note-yuan">莫候反。</sub>猗<sub class="note-yuan">乙奇反。</sub>詿<sub class="note-yuan">音挂。</sub>孺<sub class="note-yuan">而遇反。</sub>綦毋<sub class="note-yuan">音無。</sub>螟螣<sub class="note-yuan">音冥特。螣食苗葉。</sub>蟊<sub class="note-yuan">音謀。</sub>磻石<sub class="note-yuan">薄官反。</sub>螽斯<sub class="note-yuan">音終。</sub>劉勱<sub class="note-yuan">音邁。</sub>榱<sub class="note-yuan">所追反。</sub>簀<sub class="note-yuan">側革反。</sub>喧喧<sub class="note-yuan">況袁反。</sub>卿但識<sub class="note-yuan">禮記云：弟子識之。識音之吏反。</sub>

## 載記第三卷<sub class="note-yuan">晉書一百三</sub>

仆<sub class="note-yuan">撫遇反。</sub>鬚髯<sub class="note-yuan">上相俞反，下汝鹽反。</sub>菅涔<sub class="note-yuan">[^60]鋤簪反。菅一作管。</sub>掎<sub class="note-yuan">居起反。</sub>諜<sub class="note-yuan">徒叶反。</sub>大荔<sub class="note-yuan">音戾。</sub>剽<sub class="note-yuan">匹笑反，又音飄。</sub>齔<sub class="note-yuan">初謹反。</sub>滈池<sub class="note-yuan">胡老反。</sub>向魋<sub class="note-yuan">杜回反。</sub>倮<sub class="note-yuan">郎果反。</sub>咢酉<sub class="note-yuan">五各反。</sub>奮靷<sub class="note-yuan">直引反。</sub>困敦<sub class="note-yuan">丁回反。</sub>獷<sub class="note-yuan">古猛反。</sub>汧城<sub class="note-yuan">音牽。</sub>休屠<sub class="note-yuan">漢書屠音除。</sub>䯀<sub class="note-yuan">字或作𩣘。女輒反。馬行疾也。</sub>躓<sub class="note-yuan">涉利反。</sub>皚<sub class="note-yuan">五來反。</sub>軹<sub class="note-yuan">諸氏反。</sub>㡊<sub class="note-yuan">苦洽反。</sub>戲水<sub class="note-yuan">許羈反。</sub>炮烙<sub class="note-yuan">上音匏。下公百反，一作格。</sub>謻門<sub class="note-yuan">謻音池。字林力之反，又直紙反。</sub>

## 載記第四卷<sub class="note-yuan">晉書一百四</sub>

㔨<sub class="note-yuan">音背。下張㔨督同。</sub>羯<sub class="note-yuan">居謁反。</sub>鄔<sub class="note-yuan">太原鄔縣也。音一戶反。</sub>鞞<sub class="note-yuan">步迷反。</sub>鬻食<sub class="note-yuan">育、嗣二音。</sub>茌平<sub class="note-yuan">之士反，又仕疑反。</sub>逯明<sub class="note-yuan">音綠。</sub>曀<sub class="note-yuan">於計反。</sub>䐗圈<sub class="note-yuan">渠篆反。</sub>犒<sub class="note-yuan">苦到反。</sub>禧<sub class="note-yuan">許其反。</sub>敳<sub class="note-yuan">五來反。</sub>呵<sub class="note-yuan">呼何反。</sub>填殺之<sub class="note-yuan">填音陟鄰反。</sub>洧<sub class="note-yuan">榮美反。</sub>螘<sub class="note-yuan">魚綺反。</sub>愀<sub class="note-yuan">子小反，又茲糾反。</sub>鎧<sub class="note-yuan">苦愛反。</sub>筏<sub class="note-yuan">音伐。</sub>欲填<sub class="note-yuan">徒賢反。</sub>范龕<sub class="note-yuan">音堪。</sub>王眘<sub class="note-yuan">古慎字。</sub>哆<sub class="note-yuan">丁佐反，又車者反。</sub>鴦<sub class="note-yuan">烏郎反，又於良反。</sub>坫<sub class="note-yuan">都念反。</sub>桑乾<sub class="note-yuan">音干。</sub>踁<sub class="note-yuan">戶定反，本作脛，同。</sub>蛻<sub class="note-yuan">託臥反。</sub>鼂<sub class="note-yuan">音朝錯之朝。</sub>挈壺<sub class="note-yuan">苦結反。</sub>孛<sub class="note-yuan">薄背反，又薄沒反。</sub>

## 載記第五卷<sub class="note-yuan">晉書一百五</sub>

嘏<sub class="note-yuan">古雅反。</sub>滹<sub class="note-yuan">荒烏反。本或作⿰氵雩。</sub>纛<sub class="note-yuan">徒到反。</sub>呵<sub class="note-yuan">呼何反。</sub>咄哪<sub class="note-yuan">上當沒反，下乃結反。</sub>岍<sub class="note-yuan">音牽。</sub>敺<sub class="note-yuan">烏口反。</sub>漚麻<sub class="note-yuan">於候反。</sub>拳<sub class="note-yuan">巨員反。</sub>復之<sub class="note-yuan">方六反。</sub>釀<sub class="note-yuan">女亮反。</sub>㩧殺<sub class="note-yuan">蒲角反。</sub>刳<sub class="note-yuan">音枯。</sub>酇<sub class="note-yuan">才何反。</sub>酈食<sub class="note-yuan">歷、易二音。</sub>核<sub class="note-yuan">下革反。</sub>琅<sub class="note-yuan">音郎。</sub>逡遒<sub class="note-yuan">才由反。</sub>闓<sub class="note-yuan">音開。</sub>堨<sub class="note-yuan">烏葛反。</sub>澌<sub class="note-yuan">[^61]字林：澌，流水也。息移反。</sub>鞏訾<sub class="note-yuan">上居悚反，下即移反。</sub>靦<sub class="note-yuan">他典反。</sub>偵諜<sub class="note-yuan">上丑鄭反，下音蝶。</sub>句麗<sub class="note-yuan">音駒離。</sub>楛<sub class="note-yuan">音戶。</sub>于窴<sub class="note-yuan">音殿。</sub>犖<sub class="note-yuan">呂角反。</sub>礌礌<sub class="note-yuan">落猥反。</sub>洿下<sub class="note-yuan">哀都反。</sub>謏<sub class="note-yuan">蘇鳥反。</sub>湼<sub class="note-yuan">奴結反。</sub>澍<sub class="note-yuan">之樹反。</sub>涉璝<sub class="note-yuan">公回反。</sub>

## 載記第六卷<sub class="note-yuan">晉書一百六</sub>

趫<sub class="note-yuan">去遙反，又音喬。</sub>吻<sub class="note-yuan">武粉反。</sub>廮陶<sub class="note-yuan">上一井反，下音遙。</sub>費縣<sub class="note-yuan">音祕。</sub>絙<sub class="note-yuan">字林：大索也。古恒反。</sub>輞<sub class="note-yuan">音罔。</sub>橡<sub class="note-yuan">音象。</sub>綷<sub class="note-yuan">子對反。</sub>鐺<sub class="note-yuan">都堂反。</sub>崇杠<sub class="note-yuan">音江。竿也。</sub>絙繳<sub class="note-yuan">之藥反。</sub>紐<sub class="note-yuan">女九反。</sub>恧<sub class="note-yuan">女六反。</sub>褻<sub class="note-yuan">私列反。</sub>捶<sub class="note-yuan">之累反。</sub>安次<sub class="note-yuan">漢書次音恣。</sub>令支<sub class="note-yuan">應劭令音零，孟康支音祇。</sub>蹋頓<sub class="note-yuan">徒合反。</sub>海島<sub class="note-yuan">都浩反。</sub>艘<sub class="note-yuan">蘇遭反。</sub>鉦<sub class="note-yuan">諸盈反。</sub>𤟭<sub class="note-yuan">丑略反。</sub>邾<sub class="note-yuan">陟輸反。</sub>葦<sub class="note-yuan">韋鬼反。</sub>迭<sub class="note-yuan">徒結反。遞也。</sub>豫且<sub class="note-yuan">子余反。</sub>申扁<sub class="note-yuan">芳蓮反。</sub>湎<sub class="note-yuan">彌兗反。</sub>昴<sub class="note-yuan">音卯。</sub>恚<sub class="note-yuan">於避反。</sub>溺中<sub class="note-yuan">乃弔反。</sub>睕睕<sub class="note-yuan">目深也。一丸反。</sub>耐<sub class="note-yuan">乃代反。</sub>鞾<sub class="note-yuan">與靴同。</sub>偶語<sub class="note-yuan">五口反。</sub>象箸<sub class="note-yuan">張據反。</sub>孕<sub class="note-yuan">以證反。</sub>

## 載記第七卷<sub class="note-yuan">晉書一百七</sub>

湟<sub class="note-yuan">音黃。</sub>鵠<sub class="note-yuan">胡沃反。</sub>孑<sub class="note-yuan">居列反。</sub>張瑁<sub class="note-yuan">莫佩反。</sub>愀<sub class="note-yuan">子小反，又茲糾反。</sub>頷<sub class="note-yuan">胡感反。</sub>舐<sub class="note-yuan">食爾反。</sub>郝<sub class="note-yuan">呼各反。</sub>洿<sub class="note-yuan">反烏。</sub>豺<sub class="note-yuan">士諧反。</sub>頡<sub class="note-yuan">胡結反。</sub>下辦<sub class="note-yuan">步莧反。</sub>惛<sub class="note-yuan">音昏。</sub>羯<sub class="note-yuan">居謁反。</sub>盂<sub class="note-yuan">音于。</sub>燄<sub class="note-yuan">以贍反。</sub>浹<sub class="note-yuan">子協反。</sub>銖<sub class="note-yuan">市朱反。</sub>滏<sub class="note-yuan">音釜。</sub>滆<sub class="note-yuan">音鬲。</sub>譎<sub class="note-yuan">古穴反。</sub>餉<sub class="note-yuan">式亮反。</sub>縋<sub class="note-yuan">馳偽反。</sub>遏陘<sub class="note-yuan">戶經反。</sub>汨<sub class="note-yuan">音骨。</sub>聞鞞<sub class="note-yuan">蒲迷反。</sub>仆<sub class="note-yuan">音赴。</sub>倜儻<sub class="note-yuan">博雅：卓異也。倜音他歷反，儻他沆反。</sub>褫<sub class="note-yuan">池爾反。</sub>懟<sub class="note-yuan">直類反。</sub>畚鍤<sub class="note-yuan">上音本，下初洽反。</sub>惵惵<sub class="note-yuan">徒協反。</sub>獷<sub class="note-yuan">古猛反。</sub>燥<sub class="note-yuan">蘇浩反。</sub>唵<sub class="note-yuan">烏感反。</sub>

## 載記第八卷<sub class="note-yuan">晉書一百八</sub>

廆<sub class="note-yuan">五罪反。</sub>步搖<sub class="note-yuan">餘招反。</sub>魁<sub class="note-yuan">苦回反。</sub>耐<sub class="note-yuan">乃代反。</sub>膾<sub class="note-yuan">古外反。</sub>岌<sub class="note-yuan">魚及反。</sub>皝<sub class="note-yuan">音晃。</sub>崔毖<sub class="note-yuan">音祕。</sub>犒<sub class="note-yuan">苦到反。</sub>紐<sub class="note-yuan">女久反。</sub>燾<sub class="note-yuan">徒到反。</sub>傕氾<sub class="note-yuan">較、汎二音。</sub>員<sub class="note-yuan">音云。</sub>蓨<sub class="note-yuan">音條。</sub>

佟<sub class="note-yuan">徒冬反。</sub>滹<sub class="note-yuan">火湖反。</sub>蚡<sub class="note-yuan">房粉反。</sub>噂𠴲<sub class="note-yuan">上茲損反，下徒合反。</sub>漉<sub class="note-yuan">盧谷反。</sub>釗<sub class="note-yuan">指遙反。</sub>蠮螉<sub class="note-yuan">上烏結反，下烏紅反。</sub>澮<sub class="note-yuan">古兌反。</sub>墊<sub class="note-yuan">都念反。</sub>佛肸<sub class="note-yuan">許乙反。</sub>

騖<sub class="note-yuan">音務。</sub>龕<sub class="note-yuan">口含反。</sub>沲<sub class="note-yuan">徒何反。</sub>綫<sub class="note-yuan">仙箭反。</sub>瓮<sub class="note-yuan">蒲奔反。</sub>蕕<sub class="note-yuan">音由。</sub>鏗鏘<sub class="note-yuan">上口莖反，下七將反。</sub>褠<sub class="note-yuan">古侯反。</sub>襈<sub class="note-yuan">陸氏士戀反，顧音士眷反。</sub>踶齧<sub class="note-yuan">上特計反，下五結反。</sub>煢<sub class="note-yuan">渠營反。</sub>糜<sub class="note-yuan">靡爲反。</sub>鴦<sub class="note-yuan">於良反，又烏郎反。</sub>潸然出涕<sub class="note-yuan">[^62]毛詩：潸，涕下貌。劉炫音生版反。涕音他禮反。</sub>闃<sub class="note-yuan">苦覓反。</sub>蹋<sub class="note-yuan">徒合反。</sub>嵪㠂<sub class="note-yuan">口勞、五勞二反。</sub>

暐<sub class="note-yuan">文字集略：暐亦煒字，于鬼反。</sub>螗蜋<sub class="note-yuan">堂、郎二音。</sub>圈<sub class="note-yuan">渠篆反。</sub>竺<sub class="note-yuan">陟六反。一作築。[^63]</sub>跋扈<sub class="note-yuan">蒲撥反。</sub>燼<sub class="note-yuan">疾刃反。</sub>荷擔<sub class="note-yuan">都濫反。</sub>甌吳<sub class="note-yuan">一侯反。</sub>謏<sub class="note-yuan">蘇鳥反。</sub>涌<sub class="note-yuan">[^64]音勇。</sub>餽<sub class="note-yuan">逵位反。</sub>賕<sub class="note-yuan">音求。</sub>陟岵<sub class="note-yuan">字或作𱀆。乎古反。</sub>帑<sub class="note-yuan">他朗反。</sub>襜褕<sub class="note-yuan">字林曰：直裾曰襜褕。上處詹反，下式朱反。</sub>弋綈<sub class="note-yuan">杜奚反。</sub>稠<sub class="note-yuan">直牛反。</sub>彙<sub class="note-yuan">音謂。</sub>隔閡<sub class="note-yuan">五代反。</sub>挻<sub class="note-yuan">式連反。</sub>蝥<sub class="note-yuan">莫浮反。</sub>讋<sub class="note-yuan">章葉反。字林：失氣也。</sub>媼<sub class="note-yuan">烏浩反。</sub>衝輣<sub class="note-yuan">步萌反。</sub>蝟<sub class="note-yuan">云貴反。</sub>鴟<sub class="note-yuan">尺脂反。</sub>

氐<sub class="note-yuan">當奚反。</sub>酋<sub class="note-yuan">字秋反。</sub>上邽<sub class="note-yuan">古攜反。</sub>枋<sub class="note-yuan">府良反。</sub>艸<sub class="note-yuan">古草字。本或作廾。</sub>菁<sub class="note-yuan">子情反。</sub>軹<sub class="note-yuan">音紙。</sub>淅<sub class="note-yuan">音析。</sub>堆<sub class="note-yuan">都回反。</sub>屐<sub class="note-yuan">奇逆反。</sub>瞎<sub class="note-yuan">許鎋反。</sub>耐刀槊<sub class="note-yuan">所角反。</sub>跣<sub class="note-yuan">蘇典反。</sub>梁楞<sub class="note-yuan">來登反。</sub>彎<sub class="note-yuan">烏關反。</sub>錘<sub class="note-yuan">直垂反。</sub>鉗<sub class="note-yuan">巨淹反。</sub>鋸<sub class="note-yuan">音遽。</sub>僵仆<sub class="note-yuan">薑、赴二音。</sub>洿<sub class="note-yuan">音烏。</sub>鄜城<sub class="note-yuan">撫夫反。</sub>爓<sub class="note-yuan">徐廉反。</sub>隻<sub class="note-yuan">之石反。</sub>悸<sub class="note-yuan">其季反。</sub>

翌<sub class="note-yuan">與職反。</sub>嘔血<sub class="note-yuan">烏口反。</sub>蚝<sub class="note-yuan">七吏反。</sub>丐<sub class="note-yuan">古泰反。</sub>廄<sub class="note-yuan">音救。</sub>京索<sub class="note-yuan">應劭曰：京，縣名。今有大索、小索亭。晉灼曰音柵。顏氏索音求索之索。</sub>耳屬<sub class="note-yuan">朱辱反。</sub>瓫<sub class="note-yuan">蒲奔反。</sub>鷲陜<sub class="note-yuan">上音就。陜，漢書音下夾反。兩山之間也。本或作俠。</sub>漒<sub class="note-yuan">音強。</sub>肺胕<sub class="note-yuan">音府。</sub>輶<sub class="note-yuan">以周反。</sub>沮水<sub class="note-yuan">七余反。</sub>虓<sub class="note-yuan">許交反。</sub>邛笮<sub class="note-yuan">在各反。本爲莋。</sub>墊江<sub class="note-yuan">音疊。</sub>仂<sub class="note-yuan">來北反。</sub>翼犍<sub class="note-yuan">渠焉反。</sub>溉<sub class="note-yuan">古礙反。</sub>游馬以渡<sub class="note-yuan">說文曰：游水上也。[^65]音由。</sub>盱眙<sub class="note-yuan">上況于反，下與之反。</sub>溯<sub class="note-yuan">音素。</sub>璪<sub class="note-yuan">音早。</sub>涂中<sub class="note-yuan">度都反。</sub>擯<sub class="note-yuan">必刃反。</sub>褊阨<sub class="note-yuan">方緬反，烏懈反。</sub>嵕<sub class="note-yuan">子紅反。</sub>璣<sub class="note-yuan">居希反。</sub>玕<sub class="note-yuan">音干。</sub>哂<sub class="note-yuan">式忍反。</sub>於窴<sub class="note-yuan">堂見反。又音田。</sub>滶<sub class="note-yuan">五勞反。</sub>

泫氏<sub class="note-yuan">胡犬反，又胡堅反。上黨有泫氏縣。</sub>𩇕<sub class="note-yuan">音靜。</sub>朓<sub class="note-yuan">他了反。</sub>驢磑<sub class="note-yuan">五內反。</sub>讞<sub class="note-yuan">魚列反。</sub>沮渠<sub class="note-yuan">子余反。</sub>洸<sub class="note-yuan">古皇反。</sub>傉<sub class="note-yuan">內沃反。</sub>梴<sub class="note-yuan">式連反。</sub>番禾<sub class="note-yuan">音盤。</sub>譯<sub class="note-yuan">羊益反。</sub>波若<sub class="note-yuan">人者反。</sub>玁狁<sub class="note-yuan">險、允二音。</sub>鶚<sub class="note-yuan">五各反。</sub>籜<sub class="note-yuan">音託。</sub>重繭<sub class="note-yuan">上持容反，下古典反。</sub>擯<sub class="note-yuan">必刃反。</sub>

## 載記第十八卷<sub class="note-yuan">晉書一百十八</sub>

濜口<sub class="note-yuan">水經曰：濜水出襄鄉縣東北陽中山。濜，疾胤反。南都賦云：滍、澧、𤄶、濜，發源巖穴。即此水也。</sub>袤<sub class="note-yuan">莫候反。</sub>拓跋<sub class="note-yuan">上音託，下步末反。</sub>楞<sub class="note-yuan">盧登反。</sub>裨<sub class="note-yuan">音卑。</sub>平輿<sub class="note-yuan">音豫。</sub>鷲陜<sub class="note-yuan">下夾反，下同。</sub>絕亢<sub class="note-yuan">下郎反。亢，頸也。</sub>紓<sub class="note-yuan">音舒。</sub>覬<sub class="note-yuan">几利反。</sub>鏗<sub class="note-yuan">口莖反。</sub>洿<sub class="note-yuan">音烏。</sub>

雊<sub class="note-yuan">古候反。</sub>殷殷有聲<sub class="note-yuan">殷音隱。</sub>瞋<sub class="note-yuan">昌鄰反。</sub>洸<sub class="note-yuan">古皇反。</sub>完<sub class="note-yuan">胡官反。</sub>墨蠡<sub class="note-yuan">祿戈反。</sub>蠡吾<sub class="note-yuan">上音禮。</sub>掎<sub class="note-yuan">居綺反。</sub>裨將<sub class="note-yuan">頻卑反。</sub>嘔<sub class="note-yuan">一口反。</sub>拔<sub class="note-yuan">蒲撥反。</sub>憮<sub class="note-yuan">無府反。</sub>

曎<sub class="note-yuan">胡老反。</sub>碭<sub class="note-yuan">杜浪反。</sub>黔中<sub class="note-yuan">音琴。</sub>賨<sub class="note-yuan">南蠻賦才冬反。</sub>剽<sub class="note-yuan">匹笑反。</sub>復<sub class="note-yuan">方六反。</sub>覡<sub class="note-yuan">胡狄反。</sub>苾<sub class="note-yuan">毗必反。</sub>廞<sub class="note-yuan">許金反。</sub>愞<sub class="note-yuan">乃亂反。</sub>幟<sub class="note-yuan">昌志反，又式吏反。</sub>弇<sub class="note-yuan">古南反。</sub>氐叟<sub class="note-yuan">蘇后反。</sub>鞬<sub class="note-yuan">居言反。</sub>晶<sub class="note-yuan">音精。晶，光也。</sub>檉<sub class="note-yuan">敕貞反。</sub>衙博<sub class="note-yuan">上音牙。</sub>葭萌<sub class="note-yuan">上音加，下莫耕反。</sub>墊<sub class="note-yuan">徒叶反。</sub>璜<sub class="note-yuan">胡光反。</sub>氾<sub class="note-yuan">音汎。</sub>黃訇<sub class="note-yuan">呼宏反。</sub>𧢻<sub class="note-yuan">尺玉反。古文觸字。</sub>郫<sub class="note-yuan">符羈反。</sub>幢<sub class="note-yuan">宅江反。</sub>

## 載記第二十一卷<sub class="note-yuan">晉書一百二十一</sub>

郪<sub class="note-yuan">倉咨反，又音妻。</sub>芋<sub class="note-yuan">羽遇反。</sub>羅羕<sub class="note-yuan">餘亮反。</sub>覡<sub class="note-yuan">胡狄反。</sub>惇<sub class="note-yuan">都昆反。</sub>絰<sub class="note-yuan">丑結反。</sub>釗<sub class="note-yuan">指遙反。</sub>琀<sub class="note-yuan">胡紺反。</sub>玝<sub class="note-yuan">音午。</sub>扞<sub class="note-yuan">音翰。</sub>𥐗<sub class="note-yuan">字林：本砭字。萬廉反。</sub>毋丘<sub class="note-yuan">音無。</sub>朱提<sub class="note-yuan">上是朱反，下是支反。</sub>瘍<sub class="note-yuan">左傳云：生瘍於頭。字林：瘍，頭瘡也。音羊。</sub>酗<sub class="note-yuan">香句反。</sub>嘏<sub class="note-yuan">古雅反。</sub>痕<sub class="note-yuan">字林：痕，瘢也。戶恩反。</sub>吮<sub class="note-yuan">徐兗反。</sub>瓌<sub class="note-yuan">姑回反。</sub>紊<sub class="note-yuan">無運反。</sub>昝<sub class="note-yuan">子感反。</sub>艦<sub class="note-yuan">胡黯反。</sub>譟<sub class="note-yuan">蘇到反。</sub>祟<sub class="note-yuan">雖遂反。</sub>獠<sub class="note-yuan">音老。</sub>蹙<sub class="note-yuan">子六反。</sub>汶<sub class="note-yuan">音旻。</sub>鑕<sub class="note-yuan">之日反。</sub>剽竊<sub class="note-yuan">字林：剽，劫人也。匹召反。</sub>鼈<sub class="note-yuan">并列反。</sub>

砥<sub class="note-yuan">音旨。</sub>憃<sub class="note-yuan">丑江反，又丑降反。</sub>畦<sub class="note-yuan">戶圭反。</sub>撾捶<sub class="note-yuan">上陟瓜反，下之累反。</sub>鴟鴞<sub class="note-yuan">上處脂反，下于驕反。</sub>羿<sub class="note-yuan">五計反。</sub>勍<sub class="note-yuan">渠京反。</sub>呪詛<sub class="note-yuan">上職救反，下側據反。</sub>暍<sub class="note-yuan">說文：傷暑也。音謁。</sub>剗<sub class="note-yuan">初限反。</sub>躃踊<sub class="note-yuan">房益反。</sub>仆<sub class="note-yuan">音赴。</sub>襚鞾<sub class="note-yuan">與靴同。</sub>跋<sub class="note-yuan">蒲撥反。</sub>藁<sub class="note-yuan">古老反。</sub>儗<sub class="note-yuan">魚紀反。</sub>惡有<sub class="note-yuan">音烏。</sub>掎拔<sub class="note-yuan">居綺反。</sub>勦<sub class="note-yuan">子小反。</sub>鬒<sub class="note-yuan">之忍反。</sub>逭<sub class="note-yuan">胡段反。</sub>

紇<sub class="note-yuan">下沒反。</sub>汗<sub class="note-yuan">音寒。</sub>鐸<sub class="note-yuan">大各反。</sub>軻<sub class="note-yuan">音柯，又去聲。姓也。</sub>䩭<sub class="note-yuan">居宜反。</sub>翟瑥<sub class="note-yuan">音溫。</sub>狃<sub class="note-yuan">女九反。</sub>羖羝<sub class="note-yuan">音古低。</sub>踧<sub class="note-yuan">子六反。</sub>捫<sub class="note-yuan">音門。</sub>党<sub class="note-yuan">丁浪反。</sub>嵻㟍<sub class="note-yuan">康、郎二音。</sub>轘<sub class="note-yuan">音患。</sub>蹢<sub class="note-yuan">直炙反。蹢，豕蹄也。又音的。</sub>噍<sub class="note-yuan">祚叫反。</sub>阽<sub class="note-yuan">余廉反。</sub>叱咤<sub class="note-yuan">上齒只反，下陟嫁反。</sub>跋<sub class="note-yuan">蒲撥反。</sub>韮町<sub class="note-yuan">徒鼎反。</sub>閼伯<sub class="note-yuan">烏葛反。</sub>賕<sub class="note-yuan">音求。</sub>蝚蠕<sub class="note-yuan">上音柔，下而兗反。</sub>睹<sub class="note-yuan">音覩。</sub>柘<sub class="note-yuan">之夜反。</sub>鸛<sub class="note-yuan">古段反。</sub>廝<sub class="note-yuan">音斯。</sub>砥礪<sub class="note-yuan">旨、厲二音。</sub>

## 載記第二十六卷<sub class="note-yuan">晉書一百二十六</sub>

壽闐<sub class="note-yuan">徒賢反。</sub>鞬<sub class="note-yuan">居言反。</sub>湟<sub class="note-yuan">胡光反。</sub>鍮<sub class="note-yuan">託侯反。</sub>崘<sub class="note-yuan">音倫。</sub>麗靬<sub class="note-yuan">上力馳反，下音虔。</sub>頃襄<sub class="note-yuan">頃音傾。</sub>允街<sub class="note-yuan">孟康曰：允音鈆，[^67]街音皆。</sub>氐池<sub class="note-yuan">都兮反。或作丘池。</sub>番和<sub class="note-yuan">音盤。</sub>邯川<sub class="note-yuan">音寒，又胡甘反。</sub>苕藋<sub class="note-yuan">上音條，下徒弔反。</sub>愎<sub class="note-yuan">符逼反。</sub>子鮮<sub class="note-yuan">音仙。</sub>羈靮<sub class="note-yuan">音的。馬韁也。</sub>乾谿<sub class="note-yuan">音干。</sub>

## 載記第二十七卷<sub class="note-yuan">晉書一百二十七</sub>

拓拔<sub class="note-yuan">上音託，下蒲撥反。</sub>𧨳<sub class="note-yuan">音卓。</sub>紐<sub class="note-yuan">女久反。</sub>流凘<sub class="note-yuan">息移反。</sub>奎<sub class="note-yuan">苦圭反。</sub>勦<sub class="note-yuan">子小反。</sub>耿弇<sub class="note-yuan">古南反。</sub>湫隘<sub class="note-yuan">子小反。</sub>鴟鴞<sub class="note-yuan">上處脂反，下于驕反。</sub>鉦<sub class="note-yuan">諸盈反。</sub>

## 載記第二十八卷<sub class="note-yuan">晉書一百二十八</sub>

恚<sub class="note-yuan">於避反。</sub>轘<sub class="note-yuan">音患，下同。</sub>霈<sub class="note-yuan">普蓋反。</sub>峴<sub class="note-yuan">胡典反。</sub>陘<sub class="note-yuan">音邢。</sub>臨朐<sub class="note-yuan">音劬。</sub>從成<sub class="note-yuan">即容反。</sub>翌<sub class="note-yuan">與職同。</sub>龕<sub class="note-yuan">音堪。</sub>悛<sub class="note-yuan">此緣反。</sub>撓<sub class="note-yuan">奴效反。</sub>

## 載記第二十九卷<sub class="note-yuan">晉書一百二十九</sub>

沮<sub class="note-yuan">子余反。</sub>滑稽<sub class="note-yuan">史記猾、雞二音，又骨、雞二音。</sub>匄<sub class="note-yuan">古泰反。</sub>覡<sub class="note-yuan">胡狄反。</sub>挐<sub class="note-yuan">女余反。</sub>湟<sub class="note-yuan">胡光反。</sub>魋<sub class="note-yuan">杜回反。</sub>姐<sub class="note-yuan">才也反。</sub>隗<sub class="note-yuan">五罪反。</sub>澍<sub class="note-yuan">之樹反。</sub>研研<sub class="note-yuan">五見反。</sub>

## 載記第三十卷<sub class="note-yuan">晉書一百三十</sub>

孑<sub class="note-yuan">居列反。</sub>偵<sub class="note-yuan">丑鄭反。</sub>堆<sub class="note-yuan">都回反。</sub>髑髏<sub class="note-yuan">獨、樓二音。</sub>刎<sub class="note-yuan">武粉反。</sub>党<sub class="note-yuan">丁浪反。</sub>璝<sub class="note-yuan">公回反。</sub>蒯恩<sub class="note-yuan">苦壞反。</sub>罩<sub class="note-yuan">都孝反。</sub>絙<sub class="note-yuan">古恒反。</sub>濛汜<sub class="note-yuan">蒙、似二音。</sub>旰<sub class="note-yuan">音幹。</sub>綷<sub class="note-yuan">子對反。</sub>閟宮<sub class="note-yuan">兵冀反。</sub>侐<sub class="note-yuan">況逼反。</sub>嵯峨<sub class="note-yuan">上昨何反，下五哥反。</sub>螭<sub class="note-yuan">丑知反。</sub>

[^1]: 水柵　正文作「木柵」。
[^2]: 謐〔注〕名必反　此原在「皆從」上，今據正文序次移「肘」下。以下類此者均照正文序次移易，不具出校。
[^3]: 毋丘〔注〕音無　正文改作「毌丘」，「毌丘」乃複姓。毌音貫，不讀無。下同字不另出校。〇東里山人按：中華點校本改「毋丘」爲「毌丘」，誤，今皆改回。所據見：https://www.zhihu.com/question/621570064
[^4]: 鑠〔注〕晝藥反　「晝」爲「書」之誤字。廣韻作「書藥反」，經典釋文詩酌及爾雅釋詁音義俱作「舒灼反」，得音相同。
[^5]: 〔注〕說文曰㶏水出潁川　說文本作「濦」。「㶏」乃「濦」之或體，見集韻。
[^6]: 〔注〕說文渦水受淮陽浮溝浪蕩渠　說文「渦」作「濄」，漢書地理志【以後簡稱漢志】上作「渦」。「受」原作「出」，說文、漢志及水經陰溝水皆作「受」，「出」字誤，今據改。「淮陽」原作「淮南陽」，今據說文刪「南」字。
[^7]: 〔注〕漢書至浩音閤　此引顏師古漢書地理志下注與今本略異。
[^8]: 惤令〔注〕漢書惤音堅　「惤」，正文改作「㡉」。漢志上及注「惤」並作「㡉」。據說文，作「㡉」是。下同字不另出校。
[^9]: 〔注〕虞憙　本書儒林傳作「虞喜」。
[^10]: 〔注〕本或作玩　宋本正作「玩」，正文已據改。
[^11]: 涂中〔注〕音途　李校：說文涂水出益州，非此「涂中」也。廣韻：「涂，直魚切」，集韻音除，水名，與「滁」同。
[^12]: 〔注〕莊子云瞋目而語難　今本莊子無此文。商君書君臣篇有「瞋目㧖腕而語」。
[^13]: 𢁕　正文無此字，乃正文「杓」之誤字，宋本以誤「杓」爲「𢁕」可證。局本、殿本正文皆作「杓」。史記天官書本作「杓雲如繩」，當從局本、殿本。
[^14]: 厭次〔注〕一琰反　「一」原作「二」，今據帝紀六「猒次」音義改。
[^15]: 蓮勺　正文「勺」作「芍」。
[^16]: 皖〔注〕胡管反　卷上及集韻皆作「胡官反」。地名讀平聲，音桓。此作「胡管反」，「管」字疑誤。下同字不另出校。
[^17]: 函鍾　正文無「函鍾」，蓋即「林鍾」。周禮大師本作「函鍾」，何超所見晉書蓋亦作「函鍾」。
[^18]: 〔注〕小也見考工記　「考工記」下當有「注」字。周禮考工記梓人「大胸燿後」，注云，「燿讀爲哨，頎小也」。何超蓋引此。
[^19]: 菀窊　正文「菀」作「宛」。
[^20]: 裼　正文無此字，當爲「錫」之誤字。
[^21]: 熲〔注〕音炅本作穎　今各本正文皆作「穎」，不作「熲」。
[^22]: 𣄸　正文已改作「禍」字。
[^23]: 〔注〕方言帕頭　「帕頭」之「帕」，方言作「帞」，釋名、廣韻、集韻同。廣韻，帕，莫轄切，額首飾。「帕」「帞」二字不同。
[^24]: 貿　原作「𧵍」，春秋公羊傳作「貿」，今據改。「𧵍」乃「貿」之或體。
[^25]: 朓〔注〕漢書音吐彫反　漢書五行志下、孔光傳注皆作「吐了反」。
[^26]: 恇然　正文作「惘然」。
[^27]: 〔注〕左傳曰夾滍而軍至魯陽縣東　左傳僖公三十三年及杜注「滍」並作「泜」。
[^28]: 〔注〕白褒云陳蕃之子爲魯國相　漢志下「褒」作「裒」，「國相」作「相國」。
[^29]: 竊隱　正文無「竊隱」。
[^30]: 〔注〕說文至蠻夷中　說文「蠻夷」上有「北」字，據漢志下應劭注，「北」字當有。
[^31]: 〔注〕王逸云植立志也　今本楚辭招魂注作「植，志也。植一作立。」
[^32]: 吝腐　正文「吝」作「吞」。
[^33]: 〔注〕河清　「河清」非郡，乃「清河」之誤倒。
[^34]: 〔注〕周禮大司徒掌社稷之壝　「掌」當作「設」。
[^35]: 〔注〕釋名至御熱也　今本釋名釋車作「𨏥，憲也，禦熱也」。與此異。
[^36]: 荾〔注〕肯維反　「荾」即「荽」字，是心母字，「肯」當爲「胥」之誤。「胥」古文作「𦙃」，易誤認爲「肯」。
[^37]: 喣　正文作「煦」。
[^38]: 鞮〔注〕當奚反　正文無「鞮」字。
[^39]: 慷愾　正文作「慷慨」。
[^40]: 酖　正文作「鴆」。
[^41]: 珫　正文作「玩」，無「珫」字。
[^42]: 㒯　「㒯」，正文改作「曄」。參見卷七十校記。
[^43]: 〔注〕說文至發以搥敵也　說文作「發以機以追敵也」。追，說文繫傳云，左傳桓公五年釋文引作「磓」。
[^44]: 珫　正文無「珫」字。
[^45]: 賻　正文無「賻」字。有「贈錢五十萬」，或何超所見本作「賻錢五十萬」。
[^46]: 珫　正文作「玩」字，無「珫」字。
[^47]: 〔注〕說文白獸也下甘反　說文：「甝，白虎也。讀若鼏。」爾雅釋獸釋文引字林：「甝，下甘反。」
[^48]: 隧　原作「璲」，今據正文改。
[^49]: 〔注〕傳云至察也　此爾雅釋訓文。若引毛傳，當作「斤斤，明察也」。
[^50]: 〔注〕揚雄傳至音部　漢書揚雄傳「蓋」作「覆」。顏師古曰：「瓿，音蔀，小甖也。」
[^51]: 惓　正文作「倦」。
[^52]: 弩　原作「拏」，今據正文改。
[^53]: 〔注〕秭音姊　原作「下音秭」，今據漢志上注改。
[^54]: 顗　原作「覬」，今據正文及殷顗傳改。
[^55]: 畟〔注〕初力反　正文作「晏」，無「畟」字。
[^56]: 〔注〕舉足小高也　說文「小」作「行」。
[^57]: 儻　正文無「儻」字。
[^58]: 𡑌　正文無「𡑌」字，當即正文「赤埴」之「埴」字。
[^59]: 䱐〔注〕說文至兩乳　說文云，「䱡，魚名，出樂浪潘國。一曰䱡魚出江東，有兩乳」。與所引略異。
[^60]: 菅涔　正文已改作「管涔」，参見卷一〇三校記。
[^61]: 澌　正文作「凘」。說文，凘，流水也。
[^62]: 出涕　正文「出」作「流」。
[^63]: 竺〔注〕一作築　正文無「竺」字，亦無「築」字。蓋爲「慕容筑」「筑」字之形近譌。
[^64]: 涌　正文無「涌」字，疑「甬東」「甬」字之誤增水旁。
[^65]: 〔注〕說文至上也　游，說文謂「旌旗之流也」。游泳之游說文作「汓」，云「浮行水上也」。正文假「游」爲「汓」，何超直以「汓」釋之，引說文又脫「浮行」二字。
[^66]: 〔注〕音鉛㸦　「㸦」原作「牙」，今據漢志下注改。
[^67]: 〔注〕音鈆　「鈆」原作「訟」，今據漢志下注改。
